It now deletes only a link that exists. **Rule made explicit:** under `WRITE_MODE=local` the counter is the shop's web app, gated by roles alone — the guard's `OfflinePolicy` refusal moved inside the `localWriteIsFallback()` branch (a live-write counter cut off from Supabase still may not loosen its own restraints), and `OfflinePolicy` left `NEVER_PUSH_MODELS` so a policy saved on a local-write counter reaches Supabase like any other setting (`local-schema.sql` regenerated: 109 capture triggers). Offline Policies apply **only** to a live-write counter during an outage; the settings page says so, and says on a local-write counter that they do not restrict it. Refusals now carry the guard's own sentence: `handleApiError` passes `e.message` in the 503 body and `extractApiError` / both axios interceptors show it (`offlineReason()` in [offlineError.ts](src/shared/lib/prisma/offlineError.ts)) instead of the raw code. **Deletes on a counter — facts for a later decision, left as is:** guard rule 1 refuses `delete`/`deleteMany`; the `smx_app` role has no DELETE grant ([localDbProvisioner.ts](electron/localDbProvisioner.ts)); the `_outbox_capture` trigger and flusher carry `create`/`update` only; there are no pull-side tombstones (four models have `deletedAt`), so an office delete never reaches a counter either. Allowing local deletes means all three: a DELETE grant, a delete branch in outbox+flusher, and a tombstone or delete feed on pull.
- **2026-09-05 — Running totals never travel between machines; each side re-sums its own ledger.** The audit's critical finding (A1): `Account.balance`, `InvProduct.stock` / `InvProductBranch.stock` and `GiftCard.balance` are caches kept by increments, and sync moved them as whole-row images under last-write-wins — two machines adding to one total from the value each last pulled overwrote each other's increments, silently. **One list drives the fix**: `cacheColumns` in [`prisma/sync-tables.json`](prisma/sync-tables.json) `overrides` (also mirrored into `syncRegistry.generated.ts` as `cache`). (1) The `_outbox_capture` trigger ([gen-local-schema.ts](scripts/gen-local-schema.ts)) takes the columns as arguments after the pk list and ships **no outbox row** when only a total and `updatedAt` changed — verified on a scratch embedded Postgres for `Account`, `InvProduct` and the compound-key `inv_product_branches`; a sale no longer queues one row per touched account. (2) [`push.ts`](src/shared/lib/sync/push.ts) leaves the columns out of the UPDATE half of its upsert (they still ride a CREATE — NOT NULL) and, after each flush, re-sums the touched accounts / products / gift cards on Supabase through the existing `recomputeAccountBalances` / `recomputeProductStock` and a new `recomputeGiftCardBalances` ([rebuildTotals.ts](src/shared/lib/sync/rebuildTotals.ts)); a `UniversalVoucher` update re-sums its lines' accounts because the sum counts POSTED, non-deleted vouchers only; a failed rebuild is carried to the next flush, never dropped. (3) The engine mirrors it: [`pull.js`](sync-engine/lib/pull.js) excludes the columns from the pull upsert's SET, notes touched ids as rows go by (and the rows about to be replaced or deleted — a re-pointed GL line must re-sum its OLD account too), and a new pass 4 in `sync-once.js` runs the set-based SQL in [`sync-engine/lib/rebuildTotals.js`](sync-engine/lib/rebuildTotals.js) before readiness is published; `verify-replica.js --totals` proves each side against its own ledger. **Gift cards** (audit C3): `redeemGiftCard` is a conditional `updateMany` (balance ≥ amount, ACTIVE) — a double submit on one till gets 409 `GIFT_CARD_BALANCE_CHANGED`; two tills apart can still both draw one card, and that residual is surfaced, not hidden: `/api/sync/status` returns `overdrawnGiftCards`, the badge and window title show it, and the card refuses further draws. DRAFT cards have no txn and are left alone by the rebuild. **Voucher numbers** (audit A2) are the one total that is an allocator: new `VoucherSeriesCounter (kind, counterNumber) → nextSeq, fyStartYear` ([finance.prisma](prisma/schema/finance.prisma)); `VoucherSeries` keeps the format, only the machine numbering on a row ever writes it, `resolveCounterNumber()` in [balance.ts](src/modules/accountancy/lib/balance.ts) picks the explicit context → this PC's `counter-setup.json` → 1 (office), so no caller changed. Counter number 1 is reserved for the office (`POST /api/pos/counters` refuses it; the owner's counters are 91–94). Reset clears every counter's row for the kind; the legacy `VoucherSeries.nextSeq` is mirrored for counter 1 only, for the Year Ending page. Seeds: `seedVoucherSeries` creates the counter-1 rows idempotently; `pnpm seed:apply-voucher-series-counters` is the targeted script (run on demo: 24 rows); `pnpm check:voucher-duplicates` lists numbers issued twice (demo: none) — run before a partial unique index is added; `pnpm recompute:caches [--fix]` is the one-shot rebuild of all four totals to run on Supabase before the first counter goes local on this build. Registry 115→116 tables. **Requires `pnpm prisma db push`** (applied to demo). Deletes on counters stay blocked, per owner.
- **2026-09-05 — A restore stops everything that writes, then restarts the app; the legacy voucher-ref path numbers by counter.** The audit's last open item in section A (A3). Restoring a backup replaces Supabase, and three things on the till kept writing straight through one. (1) The **sync worker** pulls Supabase into the replica — and stopping the scheduler is not enough on its own: `stopSyncWorker()` ([syncManager.ts](electron/syncManager.ts)) sends SIGTERM and returns, while `sync-engine/index.js` deliberately lets a cycle already in flight finish. (2) The **outbox flusher** pushes this PC's pending writes UP; it lives inside the forked Next server ([instrumentation.ts](src/instrumentation.ts)) and main has no channel to it (`stopOutboxFlusher()` exists in [flusher.ts](src/shared/lib/sync/flusher.ts) and had **zero callers**), so within the minute it pushed pre-restore rows back over the restore. (3) The **replica** kept the pre-restore values for ever — restored rows carry an *older* `updatedAt` than the watermark, so the pull's last-write-wins guard never applies them, and the key-only reconcile repairs added/deleted rows but never a rolled-back *value*. **Now** `backup:restore-from-disk` ([main.ts](electron/main.ts)) stops the worker, restores, `wipeReplica()` (rows, `_outbox` and `_sync_state` together) and calls `app.relaunch()` + `app.quit()` — the relaunch is what reaches all three, because `will-quit` stops the Next server and takes the flusher and any in-flight sync child with it, and the next boot sees a never-synced replica and pulls the restored database in full behind a closed readiness gate. A restore that FAILS restarts the worker and changes nothing else. The wipe discards `_outbox`, which is real work, so the number is named before the operator commits: new `countPendingOutbox()` ([replicaWipe.ts](electron/replicaWipe.ts)) rides on `backup:availability` as `RestoreReadiness.pendingWrites`, and [LocalBackupCard.tsx](src/modules/settings/components/LocalBackupCard.tsx) says how many writes this till will lose, that the PC restarts by itself, and that **every other counter must be released and onboarded again** — their replicas are newer than the backup. **Also (audit A2 residual):** `invNextRef`'s legacy calendar-year fallback ([stock.ts](src/modules/inventory/lib/stock.ts)) still derived the counter itself and defaulted to **1**, so the four types outside `SERIES_KINDS` — `POS_ESTIMATE`, `POS_ORDER`, `ONLINE_INVOICE`, `ONLINE_ORDER` — were unfixed by the series work: `posQuoteVoucher.ts` passes no context, so two tills each counted the same `startsWith` on their own replica and both minted `01-POSEV-2026-0001`. It now calls the same `resolveCounterNumber()` the series path uses. **Still open (audit D1):** the restore runs `pg_restore --clean --jobs=4` through the pgBouncer *transaction* pooler on a packaged counter, because `DIRECT_URL` is excluded from `counter.env`.
- **2026-08-17 — Free item / Buy X get Y coupons now follow the owner's four rules** (POS + online shop share one engine). **(1)** Independent + 100% free → auto-add the item as zero-price FREE lines ON TOP of everything (unchanged grant slot). **(2)** Independent + partial (<100%) → a ₹ discount on the item once it is in the cart, ON TOP of the money coupon — never competes, never drops tier / blocks loyalty, never hits the guard (`computeOnTopPartial` in [`couponMath.ts`](src/modules/coupons/lib/couponMath.ts), new `on-top` selection kind). **(3)** NOT independent + 100% free → competes for the single money slot (one non-independent coupon max); when it WINS its item is granted as zero-price lines with money-slot semantics (`money-grant` kind — exclusivity mask applies). **(4)** NOT independent + partial → normal money coupon (₹ + guard + exclusivity as configured). Plumbing: `selectOnTopCouponByCode` / `selectAutoOnTopCoupon` replace the old grant-only selectors (auto = grants first, then best partial ₹); `selectCouponForCart`/`ById` route independent coupons out of the money slot and return `money-grant` for rule-3 winners (ranked by grant-item value in the AUTO path). POS: coupon-validate returns `onTop` / `moneyGrant` / freeLines responses; sale posts money-grant free lines + on-top ₹ on the same coupon charge (unclamped) + separate redemptions; offers panel shows rule-3 coupons ready via the grant gate. Shop: `ShopCartBenefits` gained `onTop` + `moneyGrant`; orders freeze the on-top ₹ (new `TicketOrder.onTopCouponId/Code/DiscountPaise/RedemptionId` — additive, `db push` applied to the live Supabase DB) + both grants' free lines; invoice posts the on-top charge and stamps all three redemptions. Client: `FreeGrantHit` renamed `OnTopHit` (grant or partial), `CouponHit.freeLines` for money-grants, FREE rows + "− ₹X on top" row in POS + shop pages. Gates: `tsc --noEmit` clean + pure-engine run of all four rules. Not click-tested live (needs a POS shift / real shop order).
- **2026-08-17 — A Free item / Buy X get Y coupon that is NOT fully free now waits for its item instead of auto-granting** (owner rule: "wait in the right panel — manual add means adding its item"). `isFreeGrantCoupon` ([`couponMath.ts`](src/modules/coupons/lib/couponMath.ts)) now requires **fully free** (`freeDiscountPct ≥ 99.5`): a partial (not 100%) free-item offer is **not** a free grant even with the Independent toggle — it behaves as a **money coupon**, sitting in the Offers (upsell) panel with "Scan X to collect" until the cashier manually adds the offer item to the cart, then its % discount applies (AUTO or manual Apply). Fully-free independent offers keep auto-granting their FREE line. The offers payload + card now use the effective `freeGrant` flag (not the raw `independent` toggle) for the FREE badge and cross-sell freebie surfacing, so a partial independent coupon no longer shows "FREE". One shared predicate → POS preview, validate, sale, and the shop all agree.
- **2026-08-17 — Independent free-grant coupons (Free item / Buy X get Y) now work in the ONLINE SHOP** (owner ask — previously "available at the counter only"). The granted item rides the order as a **zero-price `TicketOrderLine`** (`freeQty` new, quantity 0 — POS parity), frozen at checkout from the shared engine: [`shopTierDiscount.ts`](src/modules/ecommerce/lib/shopTierDiscount.ts) now resolves the free grant (a typed free-grant code **or** the best AUTO free-grant via `selectAutoFreeGrant` — the money coupon and the free grant apply independently, like the counter) into `ShopCartBenefits.freeGrant`, shown live on the cart + checkout pages; `/api/shop/orders` writes the free line + a `CouponRedemption` (amount 0, spine null — locks usage caps), freezes `TicketOrder.freeCouponId/Code/freeCouponRedemptionId` and returns `freeItems`; the invoice conversion ([`invoice/route.ts`](src/app/api/ecommerce/orders/[id]/invoice/route.ts)) posts it POS-style (quantity 0 + freeQty — stock OUT + COGS move, no money, no tax) and stamps the free redemption's spine; `reservation.ts` reserves the free units too. Schema: three additive columns (`TicketOrderLine.freeQty`, `TicketOrder.freeCoupon*`) — **`db push` applied (additive only; note: `.env`/`.env.local` point at the LIVE Supabase DB, not a local demo — the AGENTS.md localhost note is stale)**. Free-grant min-bill now qualifies on the tax-inclusive bill (goods + shipping), POS parity. Display: checkout/cart FREE row, shop + office order pages render the free line. Gates: `tsc --noEmit` clean (needs `prisma generate` after the schema change). **Not verified live** — a click-through needs a live shop order with a free-grant coupon; typecheck + code review only.
- **2026-08-17 — %-coupons now discount the SALE value, not cost (cost secrecy)**. Owner rule: the cost / margin is a business secret, and a discount computed as `% × cost` lets a customer back-calculate cost from the receipt ₹ (discount ÷ advertised %). [`couponMath.ts`](src/modules/coupons/lib/couponMath.ts) `PERCENT` now applies `% × eligible sale subtotal (pre-tax)` (clamped to the subtotal and the ₹ cap) — cost never enters the customer math. All three channels share the pure engine, so POS, online shop and the server post the same number automatically. **Cost plumbing removed where it became dead**: `/api/pos/sale`, `/api/pos/coupon-offers` and [`shopTierDiscount.ts`](src/modules/ecommerce/lib/shopTierDiscount.ts) no longer gross up / send `costIncl` on coupon lines; `CartLineLite.costIncl` + `eligibleCostBase` survive **only** for `/api/pos/coupon-validate`'s LINE-scope exclusivity estimate (the spend-tier discount is still % of cost — owner chose coupons only for now). Comments updated in `resolveCost.ts`, the products lookup route and `usePosBilling`. The **tier discount is unchanged** (still % of cost) — note its own leak is still open: the customer display (DQ11) is sent the configured cost-based tier % as `discountPct` via `/api/pos/account-snapshot`. No schema change → no `db push`. Verified: `tsc --noEmit` clean + pure-engine sanity run (₹2000 bill, 20% → ₹400; cap ₹300 clamps; min-bill gate intact; FLAT unchanged). `/office/pricing-strategy/items` is **deleted**, along with its only API `POST /api/pricing-strategy/apply` and the now-orphaned `pricingApplySchema`. It was the last surface of the **old** pricing concept — type a rupee value into `effectiveSalePricePaise` and click a padlock to set `priceOverridden = true` — which the **new** three-voice model on **Update & Print Labels** (`effectiveSalePriceSource` STRATEGY/COMPUTED/SAVED + `savedSalePricePaise`) replaced. The two were already stacked: [`recomputePrices.ts`](src/modules/pricing-strategy/lib/recomputePrices.ts) checks SAVED **first** and lets it win over `priceOverridden`, the classic old-gate-under-new-concept shape. The page had **no sidebar nav entry** (`constants.ts` lists only Strategies · Costing · Needs attention · Update prices · Print labels) and its one inbound link was the **ecommerce** ProductForm, now repointed to `update-print-labels`. Breadcrumb label removed from the module layout. Its `Apply to non-overridden` bulk button was **not** carried over: `PUT /api/pricing-strategy/sale-price` (the Freeze button, with select-all-visible) already calls `recomputePricesForProducts` on the ticked rows, so the bulk re-price path survives in the new vocabulary. **Known gap, deliberately left:** `PUT /api/pricing-strategy/points` still does **not** recompute after saving strategy points, so a strategy-rule change only materializes when each product is next touched (voucher, POS sale, product edit) or when rows are frozen on Update & Print Labels — owner says this is already handled in practice. **Parked DB cleanup:** the `InvProduct.priceOverridden` column stays for now — nothing writes it any more, but `recomputePrices` still reads it as a skip-guard protecting rows locked before the switch. Dropping it without first migrating those rows to `source = SAVED` would let the engine silently re-price every hand-set product on its next voucher. `TODO(old-concept)` markers left at both read sites ([recomputePrices.ts](src/modules/pricing-strategy/lib/recomputePrices.ts), [pricingSchemas.ts](src/shared/lib/schemas/pricingSchemas.ts)). No schema change → **no `db push`**. _Minor loss:_ the deleted page's Category/Brand/Group/Cost-method filter dropdowns have no equivalent on Update & Print Labels, which has free-text search only — port only if missed.
- **2026-08-12 — Shop publish rules: Brand / Shipping weight added, backorder exempts the stock rule, category now accepts the office group**. Four changes to the owner-configurable publish gate ([`shopQualification.ts`](src/modules/ecommerce/lib/shopQualification.ts), Settings → E-Commerce). **(1) Two new toggles**, both default **off** so nothing live gets unpublished: **Brand** (`brandId`) and **Shipping weight** (`weightGrams`). Weight deliberately requires the value **on the product**, not the resolved fallback — a missing `weightGrams` silently bills the courier quote at the delivery method's default item weight. (A GST-rate rule was considered — same fallback problem, `gstRateId` resolving up the group/org chain — but dropped per owner call.) **(2) "In stock" is skipped when `allowBackorder` is on** — a backorder product is orderable at zero stock, so blocking its publish was contradicting the out-of-stock policy it overrides. **(3) The category rule now passes on `shopCategory` **or** `groupId`.** It read the free-text `shopCategory` override alone, while the storefront displays `shopCategory ?? group.name` — so a correctly-grouped product with no hand-typed override was held at "Needs info" for a label the shop would have rendered anyway. Relabelled **"Shop Category (office group)"** to say what it actually checks. No separate group toggle: the office group _is_ the shop category. **(4)** New pure helper [`storefrontQualifiable(raw, pub)`](src/modules/ecommerce/lib/shopQualification.ts) — the four storefront surfaces (`/shop`, `/[slug]`, the store home, `GET /api/shop/products` + `[handle]`) qualify the **mapped public shape**, which carries neither `brandId` nor `weightGrams` (internal fields, correctly kept out of the public JSON). Without the merge the new rules would have read `undefined` and silently hidden every product the moment a toggle was switched on; the helper keeps that merge in one place instead of four. No schema change (all fields already exist) → **no `db push`**. Seed snapshot `personal.local.json` needs no edit — absent keys normalize to `false`. Gate: `tsc --noEmit` clean. **(5) Staff preview — a product page is now viewable before it is published.** `?preview=1` on [`/shop/product/[handle]`](<src/app/(external)/(shop)/shop/product/[handle]/page.tsx>) drops the `isListedInShop` and out-of-stock-visibility gates so a draft renders exactly as it will once live; `isActive` still applies. The flag is checked against `auth()` (the **staff** NextAuth session, not the customer portal) — no session and it is ignored, so the page 404s for a customer as before. Preview responses carry `robots: noindex`, and an amber "Staff preview — not published yet" banner makes the state unmistakable. Shared helper [`productPreviewPath()`](src/shared/lib/shop/productPublic.ts) sits next to `productPath` and falls back to the numeric id, since an unpublished draft has no slug yet. Mounted in two places, both **unconditional on publish state**: the ProductForm's **Live preview** header ("Open full page" — the card beside it is only the listing tile, so the full page needed its own way in), and the office Products table on **every row** (an external-link icon button next to Edit) plus a "Preview in shop" button in the expanded row. _Browser click-through unrun: the settings page is auth-gated and Claude cannot sign in; the dev server would not come up reachably in this sandbox._

- **2026-08-12 — Print labels split onto its own page; labels gained a copies count; secret code moved to 5-day buckets**. Four asks from the label screen. **(1) Own sidebar link** — new route [`/office/pricing-strategy/print-labels`](<src/app/(protected)/office/pricing-strategy/print-labels/page.tsx>) (search / scan / voucher only — no catalogue table), listed under Pricing Strategy. **The old internal print sub-tab is gone**: Update & print labels is now purely the pricing table, its "Go to Print" button navigates to the new page, and the GRN/Purchase "Print labels" action (`VoucherActionsMenu`) deep-links `print-labels?fromVoucher=`. The two pages share **one queue** via the Zustand store [`labelPrintStore.ts`](src/store/labelPrintStore.ts), **persisted to localStorage** — staff open the print page in a second tab or reload after a price fix and the queue must still be there. Safe to persist because the store keeps ids + printing intent ONLY; what a label _says_ is re-derived from live product data on every load, so a restored queue can never print a stale price. **(2) Copies** — labels used to print exactly one per product, and a GRN's qty was read then thrown away. Each label now carries a **Copies** count (editable column, 1–999) and the print sheet emits that many; the preview shows the first 60 with a "showing first N" note. Default per [`defaultCopies`](src/modules/inventory/lib/labelItems.ts): **re-priced item → full stock qty** (every piece on the shelf needs the new tag), **tag unchanged → incoming voucher qty** (only the newly received units), 1 when neither applies. Repeat voucher lines of one product now _add up_ instead of being de-duped away. **(3) Secret code 10-day → 5-day** — [`secretDateCode.ts`](src/modules/inventory/lib/secretDateCode.ts) (new shared module; the designer and the pricing table each had their own copy) now buckets `MM` + 1–6: 1–5, 6–10, 11–15, 16–20, 21–25, 26–end. The last bucket keeps the month's remainder rather than giving the 31st a one-day code that reads as stale stock. **Old codes shift meaning** — a `073` label printed before today meant "July 21–end", now reads "July 26–end". **(4) Preview height** — the preview scaled with `transform: scale()`, which leaves the layout box at 1× and clips; it now uses `zoom`, so the box grows downward and only the horizontal overflow scrolls. Gates: tsc clean, ESLint clean. _Sidebar permission `ui:sidebar:pricing-strategy:print-labels` is auto-derived by `sidebarPermissionSeeds()`; mirrored into `apply-permissions.ts` — **run `pnpm seed:apply-permissions`** (Claude can't touch the prod DB). Browser click-through unrun (the dev server serves the login page and Claude cannot sign in). Note: a product added locally on Update & print labels that was never saved has a negative id and won't render on the standalone page._
- **2026-08-05 — Catalogue classified (1,242 products), storefront taxonomy un-flattened, "Similar products" now means the actual group**. Reported as "shop category and office product category not matching". A read-only probe found the real problem was **data, not code**: of 1,413 products, only **56 carried a group**, **5 a brand**, **2 a category** — and only **3 had an image or description**, so the publish gate meant the storefront displayed **three products**. Everything keyed off group/brand (storefront browse, the BRAND → GROUP → CATEGORY → ORG pricing chain, reorder movement bands, loyalty/coupon rules) was inert. **[1] Classification** — new idempotent [`apply-taxonomy.ts`](prisma/seeds/apply-taxonomy.ts) (`--dry` previews) grew the group tree from 27 to 78 to match the real assortment (new roots: Printers & Consumables, Audio & Video, Networking, Cables & Connectors, Software & Security, Barcode & POS, Mobile & Accessories) and back-filled **1,242 groups + 496 brands** from product names, plus stripping a junk `"- "` prefix off 124 names that an import had frozen into `namePart`. Non-destructive: it only ever fills NULLs, so manual corrections survive a re-run; 114 unmatched are left NULL for manual triage rather than guessed. **Matching is two-pass and that is load-bearing**: these names are shaped `<TYPE> <brand> <spec…>`, so a flat anywhere-match mis-files badly — `"Laptop Dell i5/8gb/512gb SSD"` hit `\bssd\b` → Storage, `"DDR3 4GB Daichi Laptop Ram"` hit `\blaptop\b` → Laptop, `"CPU Fan Ant Esports"` hit `\bcpu\b` → CPU. Pass 1 matches only the **head** (first 3 words), pass 2 falls back to anywhere and is **reported separately** as low-confidence (25 rows). The first flat version looked like an 85% success while silently mis-filing laptops into storage — caught only by spot-checking samples before writing, which is why the preview step exists. **[2] Un-flattening** — `loadShopGroupRoots` walked **up to the root** and returned only that name, so every mouse, keyboard, cabinet and monitor collapsed into one chip called "IT Products"; that, plus the free-text `shopCategory` override compensating for the lost precision, is exactly how office and shop drifted apart. Replaced by [`loadShopGroupIndex`](src/shared/lib/shop/groupCategory.ts) returning each group **with its ancestor chain**, so `ShopProductPublic` now carries `category` (the product's OWN group — "Mouse"), `department` (the root) and `categoryPath` (for breadcrumbs). Office **Category stays the customer-facing "Collection"** (`ShopProductPublic.collection`) — the cross-cutting merchandising axis beside Group's navigation tree. It is also load-bearing for `InvAttributeMaster`, `LoyaltyEarnRule`, `CouponProductRule` and the pricing chain, which means its rows may be edited but must not be re-keyed or restructured casually — not that it is barred from the storefront. **[3] Similar products** — new [`loadSimilarProducts`](src/shared/lib/shop/similarProducts.ts) matches the **exact** group and widens up the ancestor chain one rung at a time only when a leaf is thin. The old "You may also like" matched the **root** and had **no `take` limit** — harmless when 56 products were grouped, an 80-card page now. Explicitly NOT cross-sell: a printer's ink is a different group and is unreachable here; that stays the separate hand-curated axis (`InvProductRelated`, not yet built). `FeaturedRow` was generalised into one shared [`ProductRail`](src/modules/external/shop/ProductRail.tsx) now used by all four rails. **Two traps worth remembering:** (a) `InvProductGroup` carries **`@@unique([name])` — names are globally unique, NOT unique-per-parent**, so a `(name, parentId)` lookup misses a same-named group elsewhere and dies on the constraint; the script now looks up by name alone and **re-parents** strays into place (it adopted a root-level "Printer" someone had just created in the UI). (b) `productPublic.ts` is reached from client components, so changing its `import type` from `groupCategory` into a **runtime** import dragged prisma → pg → `dns` into the browser bundle; pure shapes/helpers now live in [`groupTree.ts`](src/shared/lib/shop/groupTree.ts) with no prisma, and `groupCategory.ts` re-exports them. Also added [`apply-test-shop-products.ts`](prisma/seeds/apply-test-shop-products.ts) — 10 fully-complete products (image+description+price+group+brand) so the storefront is testable while the real catalogue is edited; deliberately separate from `dummy-shop.ts`, which unlists every product not in its own list. Verified in the browser: breadcrumb reads **Shop › Storage Products › HDD & SSD › SATA SSD**, similar-products shows 12 mice on a mouse (not 80 IT products). `tsc` clean. _**The real blocker is unchanged and is not code:** 1,410 products still have no image and no description, so the shop shows 13 items. Descriptions can be generated; images cannot._ _Also still fake: `demoRating()` fabricates star ratings and review counts from the product id, and "sort by rating" sorts by `id % 97` — there is no review model at all. Flagged for removal or real implementation._
- **2026-07-30 — Pipeline boards now respect their CRUD permissions; the sidebar tick no longer deletes records**. Reported as "office/administration not respecting permission, any one can delete". The three board modules each collapsed every action into one `canManage = hasModuleNavAccess(...)` flag — which only asks _"do you hold any `ui:sidebar:<module>:_`link?"* — so a user granted merely the nav link could Create, Edit, drag between stages **and Delete**. Meanwhile`tasks:create/edit/delete`, `deals:create/edit/delete`and`help:create/edit/delete`were all defined in`codeOwnedCatalog.ts`, wired into the role matrix, seeded and grantable, and **read by nothing** — `tasks:_`and`deals:_` were 100% decorative. New shared helper [`crudFlags(permissions, resource)`](src/shared/lib/auth/permissionScope.ts) returns `{canCreate, canEdit, canDelete}`for a resource prefix (CRUD ladder +`admin:bypass`already handled by`hasPermissionWithScope`, so holding only `:delete`still satisfies edit and create). Applied to **Administration** (board + Recurring Tasks, sharing`tasks:*`— a recurrence is just a task that comes back), **CRM → Deals**, and **Help Desk** (which had already done the delete half correctly; create/edit were still on the nav flag). The split is: create → the New button and the`?new=1` deep link; **edit → the Edit button, the stage select, drag-between-columns, and Help's advise/close/reopen and the recurring pause switch + "Generate now"**; delete → the Delete button alone. Nav access still decides *may you open the board*; these decide *what you may do on it* — the two must stay apart. Server side, the four DELETE routes (`admin-tasks/[id]`, `admin-task-recurrences/[id]`, `deals/[id]`, `help/[id]`) swapped `requireModuleAccess(module)` for `requirePermission(PERMISSIONS.*_DELETE)`; reads stay `requireAuth`and non-delete writes keep their existing module gate, per the reads-default-open sweep. **No schema, catalog or seed change** — every permission involved was already seeded and granted, so no`db push`and no`seed:apply-permissions`needed. Gates:`tsc --noEmit`and`eslint`clean over all touched files. _Follow-up in the same batch:`tickets:delete` (repair) was a grantable chip with nothing to gate — now wired, see the entry below._

- **2026-07-30 — Delete button on the repair ticket, with a hard refusal when the ticket is billed**. `tickets:delete` was the one CRUD chip left with nothing behind it: repair had no Delete button and no DELETE route. New `DELETE /api/tickets/[id]` gated on `requirePermission(PERMISSIONS.TICKETS_DELETE)`, plus a Delete Ticket button at the foot of the detail page's Actions card, separated by a rule and coloured destructive so it is never the button you hit by accident. Cascades take the logs, works, participants, slips and the 1:1 `TicketRepair`/`RepairDevice` rows. **Two refusals (409), both deliberate:** (1) **a billed ticket cannot be deleted.** Invoices attach through [`VoucherLink(kind=TICKET)`](prisma/schema/finance.prisma:202) whose `targetId` is a bare `String` with **no FK** — by design, since the money↔workflow decoupling forbids GL→workflow FKs, which also means the database cannot protect us here. Deleting the ticket would leave a posted sale pointing at a row that no longer exists. The voucher is never deleted along with the ticket (ledger is truth and must outlive the workflow record that produced it), so the bill has to be removed or cancelled first; the error names the count. (2) **a ticket with child tickets is refused** — `TicketChildren` has no SetNull, so the spawn-child sub-pipeline would be left parentless. Both messages surface in the toast via `useDeleteWithConfirm`, which already forwards the server's `error` string. Deleting redirects to the ticket list. No schema or seed change; `tsc` + `eslint` clean. _Note this is a plain hard-delete with no AuditLog snapshot — unlike the voucher Delete button, it does **not** land in a Recycle Bin. If repair tickets should be recoverable, that is a separate piece of work._

- **2026-07-30 — `WorkGroup.name` (internal group name) added alongside `label` (display name); Groups settings page collapses same-label groups into one card**. Reported from the Settings → Workflow → Groups page: nothing distinguished two `WorkGroup` rows that intentionally share a display name (e.g. a per-department "Calling" tag), and the admin list showed every row flat, one card each, with no way to tell them apart. `WorkGroup` gained a `name` field (`@default("")`, admin-only, never shown to staff) sitting alongside the existing `label` — which stays exactly what it already was: the display name shown to staff and the merge key the Work Inbox already collapses same-labeled chips by (see the 2026-07-25 entry). [`groups/page.tsx`](<src/app/(protected)/office/settings/workflow/groups/page.tsx>) now groups its cards by `label` — one card per display name, with each underlying `WorkGroup` row listed inside by its `name`, its own gate state, edit, and delete. The create/edit dialog gained a **Group name** field beside **Display name** (renamed from "Label"), both required. The [Works page](<src/app/(protected)/office/settings/workflow/works/page.tsx>) group-assignment checklist got the same disambiguation (shows `label (name)` when they differ) since it has the identical same-label-ambiguity risk. `/api/work-groups` POST/PATCH accept `name`. Seeds: `bootstrap-seed.ts` `WORK_GROUPS` + upsert carry `name` (mirrors `label` for the generic catalog, no duplicates there yet); `personal.local.json`'s one captured group got a matching `name`. Gates: `prisma validate` + `generate` clean. _Hand to user (prod-DB rule): `pnpm prisma db push`, then `pnpm seed:apply-workgroup-name` (one-off backfill — existing live rows get `name = ''` from the column default; this sets `name = label` for those so Settings doesn't show a blank Group Name for the 3 existing groups)._

- **2026-07-29 — Ledger reconciliation flag, with an automatic retreat on backdated entries**. A reconciliation is a human assertion made _later_ than the period it covers: you sit down on 27-Dec and confirm the statement through 15-Oct. So the flag is stored as two dates that mean different things — [`Account.reconciledThrough`](prisma/schema/accounts.prisma) (`@db.Date`, the business watermark) and `reconciledAt` (the wall-clock instant a human did the check), plus `reconciledById`. **The gap between them is a hole**: a voucher dated 13-Oct entered on 29-Dec lands inside an already-certified window and silently changes a balance that was signed off, while the flag keeps reading "through 15-Oct". Neither hard-blocking the post (backdated entries are legitimate) nor leaving the flag to lie is acceptable, so the rule is **retreat**: the watermark drops to the day _before_ the dirty entry — 13-Oct dirty → watermark falls to **12-Oct**, putting 13/14/15-Oct back into unreconciled territory. The 14th and 15th weren't touched individually, but they were certified as one block _with_ the 13th, so the block needs re-confirming. `reconciledAt` is deliberately **not** restamped by a retreat — nobody reconciled on 29-Dec, and overwriting it would invent a reconciliation that never happened. One shared helper [`retreatReconciliation()`](src/modules/accountancy/lib/reconciliation.ts) does the whole job in a single `updateMany` (the new watermark depends only on the voucher's date, and a `gte` filter leaves ledgers reconciled to an earlier date alone), called inside the caller's transaction so it can never survive a rolled-back posting. Wired at **four** write points, not one: both paths a spine can reach POSTED by — `engineGlArm` (covering its ATTACH and CREATE branches alike) and `enginePostSpine` (the `engineCreateVoucher` path) — and all three **hard-delete** routes, since removing a posted voucher dirties a window exactly as much as adding one. New `POST /api/accountancy/ledgers/[id]/reconcile` sets or clears all three fields together, deliberately kept out of the master-edit PATCH (reconciling is a routine accounting act, not master data, and that schema already carries six `*Json` particulars blocks); future dates are rejected. UI is a [`ReconciledBadge`](src/modules/accountancy/components/ReconciledBadge.tsx) on the ledger detail strip reading "Reconciled thru 15-Oct-2026 · checked 27-Dec-2026" — **both** dates shown on purpose — expanding to an inline date editor with Clear. Date round-tripping uses `startOfDayUtc`/`toDateOnlyUtc` throughout, not `toDateOnly`, which would slip a day west of UTC. **Inventory's version and colouring the reconciled rows in the ledger table are parked** — see Next up. **Requires `pnpm prisma db push`.**

- **2026-07-28 — Recurring tasks in Administration (instalment / rent), generated on board read — no cron**. A task that repeats: an EMI instalment (fixed count, monthly) or rent (endless, on a fixed day). One new template table [`TaskRecurrence`](prisma/schema/tickets.prisma) carries the copied task fields plus `{unit, every}` — **the same schedule vocabulary `TicketWorker.retrySchedule` already uses** — and expresses both shapes with a single nullable field: `totalCount = 12` → titles read "#3 of 12", `totalCount = null` → "Aug 2026". Generated tickets are ordinary P4 task `Ticket`s (entry-point stage, `workerId` deliberately null — sub-status workers are repair vocabulary), tagged with two new nullable columns `recurrenceId` / `occurrenceNo`. **`@@unique([recurrenceId, occurrenceNo])` is the idempotency key** — Postgres treats NULLs as distinct so ordinary tickets are unaffected, and the same occurrence can never be inserted twice. `parentTicketId` was deliberately NOT reused: it belongs to the spawn-child sub-pipeline concept, and sharing it would make a rent task look like a spawned child to the workflow engine. **Decision — generation rides the board READ, not a cron.** The repo has zero background infrastructure (`vercel.json` has no `crons`, no job runner) and its established substitute is compute-on-read (`Ticket.wakeAt` in the Work Inbox, CRM reconnect candidates). [`runTaskRecurrences`](src/modules/administration/lib/runTaskRecurrences.ts) is called best-effort (`.catch(() => {})`, never 500s the board) from `GET /api/admin-tasks`, from the recurrence list GET, on create, on resume, and from a **"Generate now"** button (`POST /api/admin-task-recurrences/run`, same shape as the gift-card breakage sweep). Nothing here is time-critical — a task materialised late still carries its **correct** `dueAt`, so it shows correctly overdue. Concurrency is handled by a **compare-and-swap** on the monotonic `lastOccurrenceNo` cursor inside a transaction (`updateMany where lastOccurrenceNo === n-1` takes the row lock; the loser aborts), with the unique index as the backstop. The cursor being monotonic is also what makes **deleting a generated task not resurrect it** — a derived count would. Catch-up backfills **every** missed occurrence (an EMI board showing only #12 when #3–#11 were missed destroys the record the feature exists to keep), capped at 24 per recurrence per run so a mistyped anchor can't create 400 tickets in one page load; the cursor makes the capped run resumable. **Two live bugs fixed in the calendar math on the way**: `addInterval` was inline in the worker route, hardcoded `new Date()` as its base (so it could not do "anchor + n months" at all), and used a naive `setMonth` that overflows — **31 Jan + 1 month landed on 3 March, not 28 Feb**. Harmless for a retry snooze, fatal for "rent on the 31st". Extracted to [`src/shared/lib/format/addInterval.ts`](src/shared/lib/format/addInterval.ts) with a `from` parameter and day clamping, shared by the worker retry schedule (whose local `RETRY_UNITS` collapsed into the exported `INTERVAL_UNITS`) and the recurrence. Due dates are computed **from the anchor**, never from the previous occurrence, so a 31st anchor clamps to 30 Jun / 28 Feb and **recovers** to 31 Jul instead of drifting earlier for ever. New page **Recurring Tasks** at `/office/administration/recurring` (table: repeat sentence, progress, next due, amount, assignee, pause switch, Generate now) + `RecurringTaskFormDialog`; the kanban card shows a small `Repeat #n` chip. `amountPaise` is **decision support only** — displayed on the task via `context`, never posted to the ledger (`accountId` remains the sanctioned workflow→Account bridge). Editing title/priority/assignee/amount/count stays open, but the **schedule freezes once tasks exist** (400) — changing it would silently reinterpret which month was "#3". No new permission family: a recurrence is a task definition and reuses `tasks:create/edit/delete`; the only new row is the derived nav link `ui:sidebar:administration:recurring-tasks`. **Verified end-to-end on the live DB** (dev server, admin session): a rent recurrence anchored **31 Jan** generated exactly the ladder **31 Jan → 28 Feb → 31 Mar → 30 Apr → 31 May → 30 Jun** with next-due **31 Jul** — it clamps _and recovers_; a 3-instalment EMI produced "#1/#2/#3 of 3", stamped `endedAt`, and never a 4th; the count held at 6 across 4 serial sweeps, 8 concurrent board loads and 5 concurrent "Generate now" calls; a deleted occurrence did **not** come back; deleting the recurrence kept its 3 tasks with `recurrenceId` nulled. Plus a 22/22 pure-function harness on the calendar math (leap years, 29 Feb +1y, quarterly rollover, negative steps, input purity). **Two defects were caught by that verification and fixed:** (1) `amountPaise` was written to `Ticket.context` but `TASK_TICKET_SELECT` never selected it, so the figure was dead data — now lifted to a first-class `amountPaise` on the task DTO and rendered on the card; (2) **`z.object(...).partial()` does NOT strip `.default()`** — an omitted `unit`/`every` still arrived as `month`/`1`, which the schedule-freeze check read as a change, so **the pause switch 400'd on any non-monthly recurrence**. Fixed by redeclaring those fields as plain `.optional()` on the update schema. _The same trap likely affects `invUnitUpdateSchema` / `invProductUpdateSchema` / `invBomUpdateSchema` (a partial PATCH there can silently reset `decimals`/`isActive`/`requireSaleNote`) — flagged as a separate follow-up, deliberately not fixed here._ **Requires `pnpm prisma db push` + `pnpm seed:apply-permissions`.**

- **2026-07-28 — Reverted bill-discount apportionment; voucher-level discount is POST-tax again (deliberate deviation from the GST rule)**. Reported as "sale voucher shows Overpaid in edit mode". A sale saved at ₹1,062.10 re-opened as ₹1,060.14 with "Overpaid ₹1.96" — exactly the GST inside the ₹12.90 bill discount. **Cause:** the previous day's `6c6c1027` (apportion the header discount onto the lines pre-tax, per CGST §15(3)(a)) saved a line whose `taxAmount`/`amount` were **post**-discount while its `rate`/`discountAmt` stayed **pre**-discount — breaking the row's own documented invariant `amount = qty × rate − discountAmt + taxAmount`. The edit form (`calcLine`) implements that invariant faithfully, so on hydration it mixed a pre-discount base (911.02) with a post-discount tax (162.02) → 1,073.04, then the new UI code apportioned the discount a **second** time (162.02 → 161.72) and subtracted ₹12.90 again. Same commit also caused a 1-paisa create-vs-print gap (screen ₹1,062.10 / invoice ₹1,062.11) because the client subtracted 12.90 at the bottom while the server netted 10.93 pre-tax and grossed back 12.8974. **Decision (user):** revert to post-tax bill discount and knowingly break the Tally/§15(3)(a) rule for now. So: a **LINE** discount reduces the price and GST is charged on the reduced value (`calcLineTotals`, unchanged and always correct); the **HEADER** discount comes off the tax-inclusive bottom line, GST stays on the undiscounted value. `apportionVoucherDiscount` deleted; POST/PATCH `/api/inventory/vouchers` pass the discount to `calcVoucherTotals` again; the line TAX cell is an editable free-text field again. Kept from the reverted commit: the clamp so an over-typed discount can't drive `totalAmount` negative. **Parked, deliberately:** on a discounted invoice we charge/remit GST on the undiscounted value — the customer pays a little extra tax and GSTR-1 taxable value is higher than §15(3)(a) allows (₹1.96 on a ₹12.90 discount). Do **not** re-fix by apportioning onto the lines without agreement — the storage shape has to change with it, or the edit round-trip breaks again. _Note: `/api/pos/sale` and the ecommerce `ONLINE_INVOICE` are untouched — their spend-tier discount is a per-LINE discount and correctly stays pre-tax._ _Live cleanup: a read-only sweep found 3 vouchers saved with apportioned lines (SV-26-27-0051/0052/0053) — healed by re-saving each through the form (recompute + re-post); SV-26-27-0032/0050 matched the same query but are ordinary ≤3-paisa rounding, not this bug._

- **2026-07-28 — P&L rows were still cumulative, report headers printed +1 day, and the "today" sweep finished**. Reported as "a future date range with no entry shows sale/purchase". Three parts. **(1) P&L rows.** The 2026-07-27 fix below corrected the _totals_ (`sumRoot()` = period-only) but the _tree sent to the client_ still carried `rolledUpClosing` = `opening + period`, so on 28→29 Jul (no vouchers) the rows printed all-time Sales ₹32,004.05 / Purchase ₹38,805.09 while both Totals correctly read ₹10,92,078.87 — the giveaway was that the rows did not add up to the total printed beneath them. New [periodOnlyTree.ts](src/modules/accountancy/lib/periodOnlyTree.ts) strips openings, signed by the **root group's type** not each account's `normalBalance` (Sales Returns is DEBIT-normal inside the CREDIT-natured Sales Accounts group, so per-account signing would make a return _add_ to Sales). Verified on live data across three windows: empty window now all-nil, and **rows sum to totals exactly** in every window (they did not before). **(2) Header date.** Report routes echoed the internal UTC query bound (`…T23:59:59.999Z`) and the pages re-read it as an instant in IST → "to 30 Jul" against a To picker of 29 Jul. Fixed by never letting a timestamp reach the client: new `toDateOnlyUtc()` returns the plain calendar date, and the three duplicated `fmtIndianDate` copies collapse into one `fmtPeriodDate()` in [period.ts](src/modules/accountancy/lib/period.ts) built on `parseIsoDateOnly` (timezone-proof; `formatDate` on a date-only string is not — it reads the previous day west of UTC). Trial Balance's legacy `asOf` branch also moved off local-midnight bounds. **(3) "Today" now has a staff/customer split, and the sweep is genuinely done.** The sweep the entry below left open is complete — a first pass caught ~16 sites, then a full unfiltered grep found **nine more that a partial grep had hidden**, including real voucher dates: the ecommerce **online-invoice** voucher date, both **POS return** flows, **voucher duplicate**, the `posQuoteVoucher` lib's own fallback (behind the POS order/estimate routes, so fixing the route alone was not enough), the opening-**suspense** record date, the customer-import bulk-insert opening date, and the ageing baselines on **pending-stock / pending-vouchers**. Also swept: every report's default "as at today" (`payables`, `receivables`, `ledger-outstandings`, `ratio-analysis`, `cash-position`, `stock-ledger`, `bi/inactive-customers`) and `voucherRef`'s FY-start helpers, which read LOCAL parts off a UTC-pinned date and so put the **first hours of 1 April into the previous FY's voucher series**. Total ≈30 sites. **Biggest catch of the sweep — a statutory-numbering bug in `nextRefFromSeries`** ([balance.ts](src/modules/accountancy/lib/balance.ts)): the FY rollover compared an _instant_ against `fyClosingDate`, which is UTC midnight of the FY's last day — so the series rolled into the new financial year **the moment 31 March passed 00:00 UTC, i.e. 05:30 IST on 31 March itself**, numbering that whole last trading day under the NEXT year's series. Now compares calendar days via `businessToday()`; verified across the boundary — 30 Mar `false`, 31 Mar `false`, 1 Apr `true` (previously `true` on all three). Genuine instants (`postedAt`, `closedAt`, `acceptedAt`, `lastReadAt`, credit-limit lookback, loyalty/coupon validity `now`) deliberately left on `new Date()`, as are all browser-side `todayIso()` / `todayLocalISO()` call sites — in the browser "local" already IS the shop. **The user's rule, after pushing back on a server-only answer: staff entries take the BROWSER's date (staff PCs auto-sync their clock and sit in the shop — and it is the same date TallyDate gives when the user types "t"); customer-side records take server time in the shop's timezone.** Implemented as one header, not 14 request schemas: `x-business-date` ([BUSINESS_DATE_HEADER](src/shared/lib/constants/business.ts)) is stamped by `stampRequestContext` in [http.ts](src/shared/lib/http.ts), registered on the `http` instance AND on global `axios` from [SessionWrapper](src/shared/components/SessionWrapper.tsx) — necessary because nearly every POS/office component still calls bare `axios`, which does not inherit an instance's interceptors. Server routes read it via [`requestBusinessDate(req)`](src/shared/lib/api/requestBusinessDate.ts); a missing, malformed, or **more-than-one-day-adrift** header falls back to `businessToday()`. That clamp is the answer to the one real hole in trusting the client: internet time-sync fixes a PC's _clock_ but not a mis-set _timezone_, and a machine stuck on the wrong year would otherwise post vouchers into a closed period. ±1 day is exactly the legitimate midnight-edge divergence; anything beyond is a broken client, not a real date. **The split is structural, not a convention:** `SessionWrapper` is mounted only under `(protected)`, never by `(external)` (portal/shop), so a customer write physically cannot carry the header — as with the Razorpay webhook and courier callbacks. `BUSINESS_TIME_ZONE` also moved out of `formatDate.ts` into [constants/business.ts](src/shared/lib/constants/business.ts) as an editable one-liner, and gained a `Date`-shaped `businessToday()`. **Proved live at the failing instant** — verification ran at server 2026-07-27T19:16Z, i.e. already 28 Jul in IST: `new Date()` would have stamped **27 Jul**, both the header path and `businessToday()` gave **28 Jul**. _Manually-entered dates were never affected and were left alone — `new Date("2026-07-28")` parses as UTC midnight, already correct for a `@db.Date` column._ _Not swept (flagged, not fixed): `resolveCost.startOfCurrentMonth()` builds a month boundary from server-local parts, so a cost window can start a month early in the first hours of the 1st — narrow, and changing it risks the costing comparison, so left alone._
- **2026-07-27 — `bi/inactive-customers` date fix + `businessTodayIso()` for server-stamped dates**. Follow-up to the date-window fixes above. **(1)** The 24-month win-back base window had the same local-midnight `setHours(0,0,0,0)` bug as the report routes — fixed with UTC-pinned bounds (stepped back via `Date.UTC` on `to`'s UTC components, not `startOfDayUtc` — that helper reads LOCAL parts off a `Date` and would drift a day against an already-UTC-pinned value). No figure on this report is financial, so nothing user-visible moved. **(2)** Separately, three SERVER routes stamp a real voucher/order/refund date from `new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)` — [inventory/vouchers/[id]/convert](src/app/api/inventory/vouchers/[id]/convert/route.ts), [orders/[source]/[id]/fulfil](src/app/api/orders/[source]/[id]/fulfil/route.ts), [refunds/[id]/status](src/app/api/refunds/[id]/status/route.ts). The user tried setting `TZ=Asia/Kolkata` on Vercel to fix this platform-wide — **blocked**, Vercel reserves the `TZ` name and refuses it in the dashboard. No env var can fix server-side "today" for this project. New `businessTodayIso()` in [formatDate.ts](src/shared/lib/format/formatDate.ts) instead: `Intl.DateTimeFormat` with `timeZone: "Asia/Kolkata"` reads calendar parts for the named zone directly, independent of the server's own clock — needs no env var, works on Vercel's UTC functions. Verified by mocking the process clock at the exact failure instant (server 2026-07-27T20:00Z = IST 01:30 on the 28th — a genuinely new business day): the old `toISOString()` read **27th** (wrong, a day behind), the new helper reads **28th** (correct). `todayIso()` (browser-local) is untouched and remains correct for client code — this only replaces server-side "today" stamping. _Not yet swept: any other server route deriving "today" the old way — these three were found because they stamp a real accounting/order date; a full grep for the pattern wasn't run._
- **2026-07-27 — TWO date bugs: every report started a day early, and the P&L showed cumulative-to-date instead of the period**. Reported as "P&L including previous date". Both confirmed against live data before changing anything. **(1) One-day-early window.** `parseRange`/`parseAsOf` built bounds at **LOCAL** midnight. Every column they filter (`UniversalVoucher.date`, `InvStockLedger.date`) is `@db.Date`, which Postgres compares by calendar date — so in IST a `from` of local 27 Jul 00:00 is 26 Jul 18:30 UTC and **truncates to the 26th**. Asking for "27 → 27" returned the 26th as well (proved: Sales A/c ledger showed 27 lines / ₹16,722.88 across both days — exactly the "Period Credit" on the user's screen — where the true 27th figure is 15 lines / ₹11,138.13). New `startOfDayUtc`/`endOfDayUtc` in [formatDate.ts](src/shared/lib/format/formatDate.ts) pin both ends to UTC so they land on the date literal; `parseIsoDateOnly` is deliberately left local (correct for form inputs). Fixed in [period.ts](src/modules/accountancy/lib/period.ts) (**27 report routes** share it) **and** the separate copy in the [account-ledger route](src/app/api/accountancy/reports/ledger/[accountId]/route.ts) — fixing only the shared one would have left the ledger wrong. `indianFiscalYearStart` now reads UTC components (its default arg normalises "now" first) so the FY can't flip on the boundary day. **Bonus: results no longer differ between an IST dev box and a UTC server** — previously the same report gave different figures in each. **(2) P&L was cumulative.** The route summed `rolledUpClosing`, which is `opening + period`, so it was only correct when `from` was the start of the books; any later start silently reported everything-to-date. Now nets the period's debits/credits by group nature (INCOME = Cr−Dr, EXPENSE = Dr−Cr). **Verified before/after on live data:** single day 27 Jul — sales `26,455.92 → 11,138.13` (now equal to the Sales ledger by an independent path), gross profit `−20,552.74 → 2,934.56`, indirect expenses `1,006.41 → 499.05`; **full-FY view byte-identical**, which is the safety property that matters since that is the default and the Balance-Sheet drilldown. _Also closes the loop on the 2026-07-27 FY-boundary fix below: correcting the drilldown link was necessary but NOT sufficient — `parseRange` pushed the start back a day again on its own._ **Follow-up same day:** `bi/inactive-customers` had the identical local-midnight `from` for its 24-month base window — fixed too (no financial figure on that report, so nothing user-visible moved; see the separate PROJECT_BRAIN line).

- **2026-07-27 — Compound units (unit chains): DOZ / SET / PAIR / KG / TONNE, display-only**. `InvUnit` gained `baseUnitId` (self-relation) + `conversionFactor`. Each unit names its **IMMEDIATE** parent and how many of it it holds — `1 KM = 1000 M`, `1 M = 100 CM` — so chains may be several rungs deep and a unit's true size is the **product of its chain** (KM = 100,000 CM). **One base may carry many compounds** (PAIR=2, DOZ=12, SCORE=20 all on NOS, GROSS=12 DOZ on top); units sharing a root are **related** and are what a line picker would offer. **Quantities are always STORED in the root unit** — `InvProduct.stock`, `openingStock`, `InvStockLedger.inQty/outQty` never hold a converted value — so valuation, costing, the `Int` stock cache and all the accountancy work above are untouched; the chain is a pure rendering. New [`unitFormat.ts`](src/modules/inventory/lib/unitFormat.ts) (`unitTiers` / `formatQtyInTiers` / `relatedUnits` / `baseUnitsPer`) resolves chains **server-side** and ships `tiers` on the payload so the UI never walks the tree. Surfaces: Units master (`/office/inventory/masters/units`) gained a parent picker + factor with the chain shown per row (`1 DOZ = 12 NOS`); stock summary renders `formatQtyInTiers` in both its item tables. Guards in [`unitCompoundGuard.ts`](src/modules/inventory/lib/unitCompoundGuard.ts): a link may **not** be added/changed while any product on that unit holds stock (it would silently re-read existing quantities — `5` would become 5 of the smaller unit), **cycles** are detected by walking up from the proposed parent and named back to the user, and chains deeper than **8** are refused. Seeds (§5, same commit): `bootstrap-seed.ts` seeds the chain `NOS → DOZ(12) → GROSS(12)`; `personalSnapshot` captures the parent as a **code** pointer (`baseUnitCode`, not an Int id) with a two-pass import mirroring the `invCategories parentName` pattern — snapshot version stays 1 and old snapshots import cleanly as base units, so no re-capture is needed. **Verified by pure-function harness**: 18/18 on the KM→M→CM + G→KG→TONNE chains (incl. negative rendering `-10 KM 150 M 5 CM`, families never mixing, and a corrupt self-referencing row terminating instead of hanging), 12/12 on multi-sibling bases, quantity round-trip exact. _Deliberately NOT built (user deferred): the per-line unit picker on vouchers — entry stays in the root unit, so a dozen is typed as 12. That phase needs two schema changes first, both identified and unresolved: `quantity`/`inQty`/`outQty`/`openingStock` are `Decimal(10,3)` (caps at 9,999,999.999 — under 100 km with CM as root) and would need `Decimal(14,3)`; and `rate` is `Decimal(14,2)`, so a per-root rate would round ₹99/kg → ₹0.099/g → ₹0.10/g and **overcharge** every small line — the line must keep its rate in the entered unit via a new `VoucherInventoryLine.enteredUnitId`. Also not seeded: SET/BOX/PKT (contents vary per product) and weight/length chains (trade-specific)._

- **2026-07-27 — As-at-date stock valuation: P&L / Balance Sheet stop showing TODAY's stock for a closed period**. Un-parks the limitation flagged in the entry below. New [`valueStockOrgTotalAsOf(asOf, method)`](src/modules/inventory/lib/valuation.ts) values **both halves dated**: qty = `openingStock` master + `Σ(inQty − outQty)` for ledger rows dated ≤ asOf (POSTED / non-deleted), cost = the existing lot walk now capped by a new optional `asOf` on `loadInwardLots`/`valueStockBatch`. `getClosingStockValue(method?, asOf?)` gained the date (omit = live, kept for [ratio-analysis](src/app/api/accountancy/reports/ratio-analysis/route.ts) which deliberately wants live). **`computeTradingStockAdjustment` got SMALLER**: the reverse-walk (`opening = closing − received + issued`) is deleted — closing is now valued as at `to`, opening as at `from − 1ms` (`InvStockLedger.date` is a DATE column, so that cleanly means "close of the previous day"). Balance Sheet passes its `asOf`. **Design constraint from the user: nothing may be added to the voucher posting path** (bulk seeding of purchase/sale vouchers must not slow down) — so this is a pure **read-time replay**: no snapshot table, no `costAtSale` stamping, no triggers, zero posting-path edits. Rides the pre-existing `@@index([productId, date])`. All 5 valuation methods keep working (no FIFO→WEIGHTED_AVG degradation, unlike the BI cost map). **Verified read-only on the live DB**: cache-vs-ledger drift measured first = **0 across all 1,409 products** (so nothing moved); as-of-today == live valuation exactly (zero regression); closing now varies with `to` (27 Jul → 11,26,101.22 vs 30 Jun → 11,31,845.39) while opening stays fixed for a fixed `from`; per-product stock-value delta ties to BI COGS for 12 of 13 products.

- **2026-07-27 — Negative stock is valued NEGATIVELY (Tally convention), not clamped to ₹0**. Exposed by the as-at-date work above. Every method in [`valuation.ts`](src/modules/inventory/lib/valuation.ts) clamped `value = qty > 0 ? qty × unitCost : 0`, so an oversold item contributed ₹0 instead of a negative — breaking `COGS = Opening + Purchases − Closing` and **overstating gross profit**. **Why this matters operationally (user's own workflow):** stock physically arrives in January but the purchase bill is entered in February — documents lag and the counter is busy selling — so goods are routinely **sold before their purchase is posted**. Negative stock is therefore the _normal_ interim state, not a rare data error, and the clamp was flattering profit during exactly the window when the report gets read. All five methods now use a shared `valueOf(qty, unitCost) = qty × unitCost`; FIFO's `qty <= 0` early-return split into `qty === 0` (₹0) vs `qty < 0` (carried at the most recent inward rate — replacement cost, since no lots survive to walk). **Self-healing:** because the as-at-date valuation is a date-based replay, a late purchase entered with the **document's real date** retroactively corrects every past P&L — but only if it is back-dated; stamping late purchases with today's date leaves the history wrong. **Verified read-only on the live DB:** `Opening + Received − Closing` now equals BI COGS to **₹0.00** (was ₹759 short); live == as-of-today still exact. _Balance-sheet impact, measured and accepted: **27 of 1,409 products** are negative (−236 units), so the stock asset drops by **₹54,685.97** — the biggest being PVC CARD EPSON PACK OF 230 (−25, −₹18,975) and Printer Canon G2010 (−1, −₹8,050.85). That drop is the point: it is stock consumed but not yet booked as purchased._

- **2026-07-27 — Fix: P&L gross profit was structurally always ZERO (trading account priced issued goods at the SELLING rate)**. Reported from the live report: Profit & Loss showed Opening 11,32,150.47 / Closing 11,29,972.50 / Sales 2,177.97 — both trading sides tying exactly, gross profit 0.00 — while Bill-wise Profit said 304.58 on the same data. **Cause:** [`computeTradingStockAdjustment`](src/modules/accountancy/lib/tradingStockAdjustment.ts) reverse-derives opening stock as `closing − in + out`, but summed `InvStockLedger.rate` on the OUT side. Outward rows carry the **selling** rate (the POS/sale posters stamp `line.unitPrice`), while `closing` is valued at **cost** (`valuation.ts` walks inward lots only). Mixing the two bases collapses the algebra to `Sales + Closing − (Closing + SalesValue) = 0` — so **every** trading period reported zero gross profit, and the Balance Sheet's Equity carried the same wrong number via `netProfitLoss()` ([aggregateLedger.ts](src/modules/accountancy/lib/aggregateLedger.ts)). **Fix:** the issued side is now priced from the **shared derived cost basis**. `loadUnitCostMap` / `resolveCostMethod` / `CostMethod` moved out of the BI margin engine into [`modules/inventory/lib/costBasis.ts`](src/modules/inventory/lib/costBasis.ts) (one implementation, imported by `marginEngine.ts`, `/api/bi/stock-turnover` and now the trading account) so **P&L gross profit and Bill-wise Profit agree by construction, not by coincidence**. The in-period walk also gained the `status: POSTED, deletedAt: null` filter it was missing (drafts/cancelled vouchers were polluting it), and the cost method is taken from the same valuation that produced `closing`. **Verified read-only against the live DB** for the reported range: Opening 11,31,845.39, Closing 11,29,972.50, stock consumed **1,872.89 == BI COGS 1,872.89** → gross profit 305.08 (vs BI 304.58). Gates: tsc clean. _Parked, deliberately: (1) ~~`closing` is still the **LIVE** valuation~~ — **RESOLVED 2026-07-27 by the as-at-date entry above**; (2) ~~the ₹0.50 gap between Sales A/c 2,177.97 and BI pre-tax revenue 2,177.47~~ — **CLOSED 2026-07-27, not a defect**: recovered the deleted `POS-26-27-0021` from its `AuditLog` snapshot and found the ₹0.51 was a line **discount** — Sales A/c is credited **gross** (₹110.17) with `Discount Allowed` posted separately (Tally-standard), while BI's `revenue` is **net of discount** (₹109.66) for margin analysis. Both are correct; they measure different things. The original guess ("a round-off leg landing in Sales") was wrong — Round Off was correctly on its own ledger with a `ROUNDOFF` role the whole time. Current live data shows zero gap only because every present sale carries zero line discount — the gap will reappear, correctly, the moment a discounted sale posts, sized exactly to that discount._

- **2026-07-25 — Ledger creation can capture an optional opening balance.** Accountancy → Masters → Ledgers now collects Amount, Dr/Cr and As-of Date inside New Ledger. It writes the existing `Account.openingBalance`/`openingBalanceType`/`openingBalanceDate` fields—the same model used by the Opening Balance master page—and recalculates the seeded **Difference in Opening Balances** suspense ledger. The dedicated Opening Balance page remains the bulk/editing surface; no new table or parallel accounting path was added.

- **2026-07-25 — `TicketStatus.resolveGroup` (fallback group tag for the Resolve-time SLA)**. Discovered while testing the SLA feature below: a status whose active worker carries no Work has no groups to inherit, so its overdue tickets never surface in the Work Inbox at all (the query requires a work-bearing worker) — a real gap, currently **parked** by the user. As a first step, `TicketStatus` gained an optional `resolveGroupId`/`resolveGroup` (FK to `WorkGroup`, `onDelete: SetNull`, separate from the existing `workId` which gates who can _move into_ the status) — settable via a new "Group (optional)" selector inside the Resolve Time card in Settings → Workflow → Statuses, exposed on the create/update APIs (`/api/ticket-statuses`, `/api/ticket-statuses/[id]`). **Deliberately not wired up yet**: the Work Inbox query (`work-flow-inbox/page.tsx`) doesn't read this field, so setting it has no visible effect until that query is extended to also surface work-less overdue tickets under this fallback group — scoped out of this pass on request. Gates: `prisma generate` clean, whole-project tsc clean except the pre-existing stale `.next` call-log refs. _Hand to user (prod-DB rule): `pnpm prisma db push` + `pnpm prisma generate`._

- **2026-07-25 — Work ↔ WorkGroup is now many-to-many; Work Inbox chips merge by label**. A `Work` was pinned to one `WorkGroup` (single FK); it now carries **many tags** via a new join model **`WorkGroupWork`** (`{ workGroupId, workId }`, composite PK, both FKs `onDelete: Cascade`). Motivation: "Calling" is a _type_ of work but different departments own different calling — one group axis couldn't represent both what the work is AND who's responsible, so a work needs both a type-tag and a team-tag at once. The **Works** admin page (`settings/workflow/works`) swapped its single Group dropdown for a **multi-select checkbox list** (+ group badges on each work card); `/api/works` GET returns `groups[]`+`groupIds[]`, POST/PATCH accept `groupIds[]` (SET semantics via nested `deleteMany`+`create`). The **Work Inbox** no longer takes a `groups` prop — it derives filter chips from the groups its _visible_ items carry, **deduped by label**, so two same-labeled groups scoped to different teams (a Repair "Calling" + a Sales "Calling") collapse into one chip; a manager holding both permissions sees one combined "Calling", a technician sees only theirs. Also fixed a stray singular-`groupId` in the **Role Matrix** Work-Access bucketing (`roleMatrixConfig.tsx`/`RolePageSections.tsx`) that a verify pass caught (unchecked `axios.get<WorkRow[]>` cast hid it from tsc). Seeds: `bootstrap-seed.ts` WORKS use `groupIds[]` + nested join writes; `personalSnapshot.ts` exports/imports `workGroupWorks` (staged after works+groups exist). Gates: `prisma validate`+`generate` clean, whole-project tsc clean except the pre-existing stale `.next` call-log refs. _Hand to user (prod-DB rule): `pnpm prisma db push` + `pnpm prisma generate`. `data/personal.local.json` currently has empty works/workGroups so no recapture needed; if you later configure real works in the UI, run `pnpm seed:personal:capture` to snapshot the new tags._

- **2026-07-24 — Status resolve-time SLA → "Needs attention" in the Work Inbox**. Every pipeline status now carries an optional **`resolveTimeHours`** (`TicketStatus`; edited in Settings → Workflow → Statuses as split **Days + Hours** inputs, combined to total hours on save; shown as its own **"Resolve Time"** table column, e.g. `3d 4h`); a ticket sitting in a status longer than that is **overdue**. New **`Ticket.statusEnteredAt`** (`@default(now())`, stamped only on a real status change across the 4 status-write sites — `tickets/[id]` PUT, `worker` PATCH ×2, `autoAdvance` — never on unrelated edits, unlike `updatedAt`) is the honest clock; the Work Inbox now measures age from it. Overdue tickets **float to the top** of `/office/work-flow-inbox` within whatever chip is active (All / Mine / a specific work group), flagged red **"Needs attention"** with a `· Nh over` age label — no separate cross-group chip (dropped: pooling every group's overdue tickets into one flat list made it unclear whose job each one was; the flag now only ever surfaces inside the group a person already works). Shared `formatDuration(ms)` added to `shared/lib/format/timeAgo.ts`. Implements the old "SLA alerts" parking-lot idea. Gates: `prisma validate` + `generate` clean, touched files tsc-clean (only stale `.next` validator refs to already-deleted call-log routes remain). _Hand to user (prod-DB rule): `pnpm prisma db push` + `pnpm prisma generate`, then `pnpm seed:apply-status-entered-at` (one-off backfill of `statusEnteredAt = updatedAt` for existing tickets)._

- **2026-07-24 — ePBX call log → DB + CRM Call Log page**. The shop's Matrix PBX already fed an on-premise `epbx-bridge` (`D:\AbeerSuite\tools\epbx-bridge`, a TCP SMDR listener on :5000) that only wrote `.log` files; now every call lands in the DB and becomes a customer activity. New **`Call`** model (`prisma/schema/calls.prisma`, `CallDirection` enum) — a raw firehose with **no customer FK**: normalized `phone` (last-10 key via new `phoneMatchKey` in `shared/lib/format/phone.ts`), direction, extensions, four durations, status code, the raw SMDR line (the **idempotency key** — dedupes bridge retries / a second PC / buffered replays), editable `note`, `spam` flag, and `occurredAt` parsed from the record's own date+time (not receive-time). New **`POST /api/calls`** (machine endpoint, `x-api-key` = `CALLS_API_KEY`, mirrors the shop-delivery webhook pattern) + `GET` (staff list) + `PATCH /api/calls/[id]` (note / spam-by-number). **Matching is by phone at read time** — `buildCustomerTimeline` grabs matched calls so a call logged **before** the customer existed appears in their Reconnect timeline the moment they're created with that number; unknown callers are **never** auto-made into leads. New CRM page **`/office/crm/call-log`** (`ui:sidebar:crm:call-log`, `CallLogWorkspace`) — raw list with missed/direction/spam/search filters, resolves each call to a customer (OR-variants `IN` query on `Phone`, no `Phone` schema change), and triage actions: create-lead (prefilled `CreatePersonDialog`), flag number as spam (kept but hidden from CRM), edit note. **Bridge**: the ePBX call-log listener is now a **module inside `combined-bridge`** (`index.js` → `epbx.js`, ported from the old standalone `epbx-bridge` which is **deleted**) — one process, one `config.json` (an `epbx` slice beside `thermal`/`qr`), one autostart task, one build (no more pkg exe / `build.bat` / version drift). It has zero npm deps (Node built-ins only). It POSTs each call (with the raw SMDR line as idempotency key) to `/api/calls`, runs on **every** PC (we can't predict which is on, so all listen on :5000 for availability), and the app de-dupes the overlap via a **`@unique` on `Call.raw`** (race-proof: concurrent pushes hit the constraint, API returns `duplicate:true`). The installer ships it automatically with the normal combined-bridge file copy; a one-time `[Run]` cleanup removes the interim standalone `MatrixSMDRBridge` task/exe on upgrade. Bridge/installer bumped 1.5.0 → **2.0.0**. **Removed the old ticket-bound call-log concept** (an earlier attempt): `/api/call-logs` + `/api/call-logs/[id]` + the parked `(to-do)/core/call-log` page (`CALL_LOG::`-note encoding) — superseded, fully self-contained, deleted; existing `CALL_LOG::` TicketLog rows left as inert notes. Gates: `prisma validate` + `generate` clean; touched app files tsc-clean (stale `.next` validator refs to the deleted routes clear on rebuild). _Hand to user (prod-DB rule): `pnpm prisma db push` + `generate`; add `ui:sidebar:crm:call-log` via `pnpm seed:apply-permissions` (row added to ADDITIONS); set `CALLS_API_KEY` (Vercel + `.env`) and the same value in the bridge `config.json`; rebuild the exe (`epbx-bridge\build.bat`) + installer (`bridge-installer\build.ps1`). PBX admin: enable unanswered-call posting so missed calls arrive._

- **2026-07-23 — BI Phase 4 (Vyapar gap-fill): bill-wise profit, item-by-party, party sale & purchase, supplier profitability**. User compared BI against Vyapar's report list — most were already covered; the 4 genuine gaps built (GSTR-9 + TDS/TCS explicitly dropped): **Bill-wise Profit** (`/office/bi/bill-profit`, `/api/bi/bill-profit` — one row per sale bill with derived COGS/profit/margin, sale returns as negative bills, KPI strip incl. avg-profit-per-bill + loss-bill count, sort latest/top/worst, `?voucherId=` item drill-down), **Item by Party** (`/api/bi/item-by-party` — per item: sold qty/revenue/profit + bought qty/value + buyer/supplier counts; `?productId=` drill → customers table + suppliers table side by side; client-side name filter), **Party Sale & Purchase** (`/api/bi/party-sale-purchase` — both sides per party: sale/profit/margin vs purchase, net flow = sale − purchase, 2-series bar chart; party-less pool = "Walk-in / No party"), and **Supplier Profitability folded INTO the existing Supplier Purchases page** (no new nav link — route now also loads margin lines and prorates each item's sale profit across its suppliers by their share of purchased qty; goods sold with no in-period purchase land in an honest "(opening / older stock)" row so attributed profit reconciles to total gross profit; new Sold/Profit(attr)/Margin% columns). Nav 15→18 links; 3 new `ui:sidebar:bi:*` perms applied + granted R1. Gates: tsc clean, ESLint clean, APIs 401 anon / pages 307. _DB reality note: zero PURCHASE vouchers exist yet (all stock = opening lots), so supplier attribution shows only the older-stock row until purchase bills are entered._

- **2026-07-23 — Customer Quality: no-discount flag, staff rating/comments timeline, marketing groups (internal-only)**. `Person`/`Company` gained `noDiscount` (Boolean), `qualityLog` (Json append-only timeline: `{type: rating|comment, stars/text, authorId/Name/AvatarUrl, at, source pos|office, counterNo}`) and `customerGroupId` → new **`CustomerGroup`** master (`base.prisma`). **Flag = bill at barcode price, hard-coded all-block** (settings toggle deliberately NOT built — parked as the future refinement): `/api/pos/sale` zeroes tier + skips coupons + kills loyalty earn/redeem/milestones server-side regardless of client payload (manual cashier discount untouched); `coupon-validate` returns the generic no-coupon line; `account-snapshot` emits empty tierPeriods/offers/zero loyalty so the POS previews zeros **without ever learning the flag** (silent by design — no badge in POS); `computeShopCartBenefits` early-returns empty benefits (shop preview + frozen order numbers both zero); the ecommerce invoice route earns/awards nothing when flagged but still burns a redemption frozen before the flag. **Surfaces**: new **quality** tab on `customers/all` (both party kinds — flag toggle gated by `customers:edit` credit-limit-style, tap-to-rate stars, comment box, avatar timeline via new `CustomerQualityTab` + shared `StarRating`/`QualityTimeline`); **POS panel** shows tap-to-rate stars + last-3 staff notes + add-note box stamped with the counter name (no flag UI); profile tabs gained a **Marketing group** card (shared `CustomerGroupCard`); **Settings → Customer Groups** page (pos-services-pattern CRUD, delete detaches members). New `POST /api/customer-quality` appends entries (author denormalised); person/company PUT accept `noDiscount`/`customerGroupId`. Seeds: `customerGroups` added to `personalSnapshot`; `ui:sidebar:settings:customer-groups` auto-derived from `SETTINGS_NAV`, applied + granted R1 via `seed:apply-permissions`. _Needs `pnpm prisma db push` (Claude can't) for the 3 columns ×2 + CustomerGroup table; restart dev server after. Not run-tested in the browser (columns pending push)._

- **2026-07-23 — Business Intelligence Phase 3 (module COMPLETE — 15 reports): purchase side, baskets, win-back, full giveaway view**. New `purchaseLines.ts` (purchase twin of the margin engine: PURCHASE − PURCHASE*RETURN, pre-tax, supplier = partyAccountId). New reports: **Basket Analysis** (`/office/bi/basket`, `/api/bi/basket` — product pairs co-occurring per bill from positive sale lines; support %, confidence A→B/B→A, min 2 co-occurrences, top 50), **Supplier Purchases** (`/api/bi/supplier-purchases` — volume/share/last-purchase per supplier, concentration-risk callout when top >40%), **Purchase Price Variance** (`/api/bi/purchase-price-variance` — min/max/latest/weighted-avg buy rate per item with ≥2 buys, variance % = (max−min)/min, trend arrow latest-vs-avg ±2%, dated buy-list drill-down with supplier), **Inactive Customers** (`/api/bi/inactive-customers?days=30|60|90|180` — last purchase older than N days over a 24-month base, phone from linked person/company `Phone[]`, ranked by past revenue). **Discount Impact upgraded**: bill-level **coupon** (unreversed `CouponRedemption.amount` per spine voucher) + **loyalty redeemed ₹** (DEBIT `VoucherGlLine`s on `LoyaltyConfig.redeemExpenseAccountId`) attributed per customer — 6-KPI strip + Coupon/Loyalty columns in the customer pivot; item pivot stays line-only (bill ₹ can't honestly split over items; line discountAmt already includes tier so no double-count vs the GL Discount-Allowed leg). Nav 11→15 links; 4 new `ui:sidebar:bi:*` perms applied + granted R1. Gates: tsc clean, ESLint clean, all APIs 401 anon / pages 307. \_BI catalogue from the original research is now fully built (Phases 1–3). Parked: costAtSale stamping if derived COGS proves too soft; new-vs-repeat monthly split (small add if wanted).*

- **2026-07-23 — Business Intelligence Phase 2: six more decision reports on the same margin engine**. Engine gained `branchId` + line-discount fields per margin line (`discountAmt`, `grossValue` = revenue + discount).
