New reports: **RFM Segmentation** (`/office/bi/rfm`, `/api/bi/rfm` — R/F/M quintile scores 1–5 within the customer base, segments Champion/Loyal/Big Spender/New/At Risk/Lost/Regular, walk-in excluded, default window last 12 months, clickable segment tiles), **ABC Analysis** (`/office/bi/abc` — client-side over `/api/bi/item-profit`; A=80%/B=95% cutoff constants, rank by revenue or profit, ≤0-metric rows unclassified "—", Pareto), **Stock Turnover** (`/api/bi/stock-turnover` — value-based: period COGS ÷ avg inventory value at derived unit cost, days-of-inventory, item/category pivot, slowest-first default), **Sales Trend & Seasonality** (`/api/bi/sales-trend` — monthly this-year vs same-months-last-year two-line chart + YoY % table + Mon–Sun day-of-week revenue bars), **Branch Comparison** (`/api/bi/branch-comparison` — per-branch revenue/share/profit/margin/avg-bill from margin-line branchId, "(no branch)" pool, grouped 2-series bars), **Discount Impact** (`/api/bi/discount-impact` — LINE-level discounts only; gross vs discount vs margin kept, by item/customer, rows where disc% > margin% flagged red; bill-level coupon/loyalty noted for Phase 3). Components: `TrendChart` generalized to arbitrary 2-series, new `SimpleBarChart` (vertical 1–2 series). Nav 5→11 links; 6 new `ui:sidebar:bi:*` perms applied + granted R1. Gates: tsc clean, ESLint clean, all 5 new APIs 401 anon / pages 307 on the dev server. _Phase 3 remaining: basket analysis, supplier/purchase-price reports, inactive customers, bill-level discount rows._

- **2026-07-23 — Business Intelligence module (Phase 1): management decision reports, distinct from Accountancy's operational reports**. New module `bi` — sidebar section **Business Intelligence** (`/office/bi` dashboard + 5 report pages), APIs under `/api/bi/*` (requireAuth reads, per the open-reads rule; no capability perms — only the auto-derived `ui:sidebar:bi:*` visibility ids, applied to DB + granted to R1 via `seed:apply-permissions`). **Core = the margin engine** ([marginEngine.ts](src/modules/bi/lib/marginEngine.ts)): since **no cost is stored at sale time**, per-unit COGS is derived at report time from inward `InvStockLedger` lots **date-capped at the period end** (same lot semantics as `valuation.ts`; org method honoured, FIFO/MONTHLY*AVG fall back to WEIGHTED_AVG for COGS; opening-rate → no-cost-basis fallback flagged in the UI). Sales universe = `SALE`+`POS`+`ONLINE_INVOICE` minus `SALE_RETURN`; revenue is **pre-tax after discount** (`amount − taxAmount`); free qty costs COGS but earns ₹0; walk-in POS pools as "Walk-in / Cash". **Reports:** Customer-wise Profit (item drill-down), Top Customers (rank by profit/revenue/frequency, share % + cumulative, Pareto on one %-axis), Item-wise Profit (pivot item/category/brand, below-cost rows flagged red), FSN & Dead Stock (turnover = out ÷ avg stock, Fast ≥ 3 constant `FAST_MIN_TURNOVER`; non-moving with stock = dead stock + locked value via `valueStockBatch`), Item Movement Register (opening/in/out/closing + dated voucher ledger drill-down). **Charts = recharts** (new dep) with a dataviz-validated 2-slot palette (light+dark, CVD-safe) in shared components (`TrendChart`/`TopBarChart`/`ParetoChart` + `BiFilterBar`/`KpiCard`, `useBiQuery`). Gates: tsc clean, ESLint clean; APIs verified 401 anon on the dev server; **margin engine run against the real DB read-only** (90d: 10 lines, ₹6,988 revenue, ₹2,067 profit, 29.6% margin, WEIGHTED_AVG) — authenticated page click-through pending (login is the user's). \_Phase 2 planned: RFM segmentation, ABC analysis, stock-turnover report, sales trend/seasonality, branch comparison, discount impact. Phase 3: basket analysis, supplier/purchase-price reports, inactive customers. Parked: stamping `costAtSale` on sale lines (schema + backfill) if derived COGS ever proves too soft.*

- **2026-07-23 — Two new checkout delivery kinds: Pickup from store + Free delivery (courier catalog 4→6)**: the hard-coded catalog ([deliveryMethodConfig.ts](src/modules/delivery/lib/deliveryMethodConfig.ts)) gained **`STORE_PICKUP`** (always ₹0; customer collects from the shop; `configJson.note` = store address/timings shown under the option at checkout and on the shop order page, snapshotted as `shippingAddress.pickupNote`; also has an optional `configJson.include` serve area — **empty = offered everywhere** (pickup costs nothing), non-empty = offered only where it matches — verified via tsx round-trip: empty→Delhi shows pickup, serve-Assam→Delhi hides it) and **`FREE_DELIVERY`** (₹0, offered ONLY where its **serve list** `configJson.include` `{states, pincodePrefixes}` matches — same shape/sanitizer as the common `exclude`, matched by the shared `destinationMatchesList`; empty serve list = offered nowhere; `exclude` blocks pockets inside a served state, e.g. serve Assam block 7844\*). Defaults: pickup active, free delivery **inactive with empty coverage** (safety). Quote path pushes both outside the aggregator curation (₹0 → price-sort puts them on top). Settings → Couriers renders both cards self-healed (create-if-absent on load — no seed script needed; both bootstraps already read `DELIVERY_METHOD_DEFAULTS`), with a pickup-note editor and a serve-area editor (state multi-select + prefix textarea, mirroring exclusions). DeliveryBoard got labels for the new carrier values (legacy manual `PICKUP` value kept as a flagged bridge). Gates: ESLint clean; **tsx round-trip verified on the dev DB** — served Assam pin shows both ₹0 options, `7844*` blocked pin and Delhi hide free delivery, pickup always present; test config restored. _Parked: office fulfilment flow treats pickup like any delivery ticket (no courier assign needed — staff just mark handed over)._

- **2026-07-23 — Fix: welcome tier re-firing for returning shop customers**: a customer whose paid orders were never invoiced has `invoiceCount 0` (spend brackets read POSTED vouchers only — ledger = truth), so NEW-scoped welcome periods fired on every order. [tierSpend.ts](src/shared/lib/pricing/tierSpend.ts) now takes `opts.extraPriorSales` and exposes `isNewCustomer` on the context; [shopTierDiscount.ts](src/modules/ecommerce/lib/shopTierDiscount.ts) counts the account's prior **PURCHASED** orders (`legacyStatus` PAID/PACKED/SHIPPED/DELIVERED/REFUNDED — an abandoned PENDING*PAYMENT checkout must NOT cost a first-timer their welcome offer) and feeds it in (coupon NEW/RETURNING eligibility reads the same verdict). Verified against the live account (4 PAID + 10 PENDING orders → isNewCustomer false, welcome gone; a pending-only account stays new). POS account-snapshot path unchanged (no opts → posted-voucher behaviour). \_Operational note: bracket levels (Bronze/Silver/…) only score once orders are INVOICED — uninvoiced history earns nothing.*
- **2026-07-23 — Loyalty online (Phase 3, closes "every POS discount online")**: [shopTierDiscount.ts](src/modules/ecommerce/lib/shopTierDiscount.ts) now finalizes **tier + coupon + loyalty** together. **Redeem at checkout**: the checkout shows the live balance + "Use points" box; requested points ride the preview + order payloads and are clamped server-side by POS's `clampRedemption` (balance ∧ min-points/min-invoice floors ∧ the cart-aware per-line redeem-cap ceiling via `redeemableBaseForCart` — same earn-rule index + taxonomy chains as POS). Frozen as `TicketOrder.loyaltyRedeemPoints/loyaltyRedeemPaise`; `totalPaise` net → gateways/COD charge the reduced amount. Points are **validated only** at checkout — no burn. **Interplay**: explicit non-stackable code + points → hard 422 (POS parity); non-stackable AUTO offer + points → offer silently skipped (the customer chose their points); stackable coupons pass `loyaltyRupees` into `finalizeCouponAmount`. Ledger guard: `LOYALTY_LEDGER_MISSING` 422 at checkout when the redeem-expense ledger is unseeded. **Invoice conversion**: replays the redemption as the POS negative charge row, then in the posting tx runs `applyLoyaltyForSale` (burn FIFO lots + **earn** on goods with `combinedDiscount` = tier+coupon+redemption, min-earn-invoice floor on the pre-discount bill) + `applyMilestones` (lifetime spend counts ALL sale channels) — guarded by 422 `LOYALTY_POINTS_GONE` if the balance was drained between checkout and invoicing. POS-side consistency fixes in the same change: the sale route's coupon `isNewCustomer` check + milestone lifetime-spend agg now count `ONLINE_INVOICE` too (`TIER_SPEND_VOUCHER_TYPES`). Portal/office order pages + printed ladder show the Loyalty row (ladder classification pre-existed). Gates: tsc clean, ESLint clean, check-docs green. _Same `pnpm prisma db push` covers the 2 new loyalty columns (11 total pending). Parked: releasing frozen points/coupon slots on order cancellation (cancel-flow phase); gift cards online (indefinite)._
- **2026-07-23 — Coupons online (Phase 2 of "every POS discount online")**: the shared POS coupon engine now serves the shop. [shopTierDiscount.ts](src/modules/ecommerce/lib/shopTierDiscount.ts) grew into `computeShopCartBenefits` — tier + coupon finalized together via `finalizeCouponAmount` (non-stackable coupon **drops the tier**; global discount guard clamps the coupon; loyaltyRupees=0 until Phase 3). Coupon source = typed code (new code box on checkout, uppercased; explicit-code failure = 422/inline error) or the **best AUTO offer** (channel `SHOP`, silent, shown on cart as "Offer applied (name)"). `/api/shop/tier-preview` accepts `couponCode` and returns `{tier, coupon, couponError}`; `/api/shop/orders` freezes `TicketOrder.couponId/couponCode/couponDiscountPaise` (codeless AUTO offers freeze their name) and takes the **usage slot at checkout** — `CouponRedemption` (channel SHOP, `universalVoucherId` null) written in the same tx as the order, id stored as `couponRedemptionId`; the **invoice conversion stamps the spine** onto it and replays the coupon as the POS-style negative charge (Dr coupon expense ledger; 422 `COUPON_LEDGER_MISSING` when unconfigured — checkout also refuses typed codes then). `totalPaise` net of tier+coupon → gateways/COD charge the reduced amount unchanged; printed ladder shows the coupon row automatically (settlement-charge classification already existed). Portal + office order pages show the Coupon row. Gates: tsc clean, ESLint clean, check-docs green. _Same `pnpm prisma db push` covers the 4 new coupon columns. Parked: releasing a checkout-taken usage slot when an order is cancelled/abandoned (redemption row currently stays; reverse manually or wait for the cancel-flow phase); Phase 3 loyalty online next._
- **2026-07-23 — Online channel = POS benefits + POS structure (tier discount online, ONLINE_INVOICE rebuilt POS-style, POS-format parcel print)**: Phase 1 of "every POS discount online". **Tier discount at checkout**: shared server resolver extracted from account-snapshot into [tierSpend.ts](src/shared/lib/pricing/tierSpend.ts) (`resolveTierForAccount`; bracket spend now counts `SALE`+`POS`+**`ONLINE_INVOICE`** — one ladder across channels) + [shopTierDiscount.ts](src/modules/ecommerce/lib/shopTierDiscount.ts) (POS formula: tier% × cost-excl × qty, pre-tax, clamped; inclusive effect backed out per product GST). `/api/shop/orders` resolves it server-side and **freezes** it (`TicketOrder.tierDiscountPaise/Pct/tierLabel`, per-line `TicketOrderLine.tierDiscountPaise`; `totalPaise` stored NET → Razorpay/PayU/Cashfree/COD all charge the discounted amount untouched). Cart + checkout show a live "Level discount (Gold)" row for logged-in customers via `POST /api/shop/tier-preview` (same helper — preview can't drift); portal order page + office order page show the frozen row. **Invoice conversion rebuilt** ([invoice/route.ts](src/app/api/ecommerce/orders/[id]/invoice/route.ts)): engine-direct like /api/pos/sale (spine → detail → lines → `saleToSpec`/`enginePost`, stock OUT in the same tx — the old HTTP self-calls to /api/inventory/vouchers are gone). Posts POS-shape: gross subtotal + per-line netted tier discount + **Discount Allowed** leg, **shipping GST** (composite supply, `resolveShippingGstRate` — closes the parked "ecommerce shipping untaxed" item), ≤5p Round-Off snap so invoice total == paid total exactly (AR nets to zero), ladder snapshots (tag + MRP) on lines. **Print**: new `ONLINE_INVOICE` template kind (catalog + bootstrap auto-included; live row seeded via temp script as a **duplicate of the live POS row**, headerLine2 "Tax Invoice (Online Order)", default A4) — `/receipt/invoice/[id]?kind=ONLINE_INVOICE` renders the POS customer ladder (isSaleKind extended); Print button on the office order page (parcel insert); registers' thermal href routes it like POS. Gates: tsc clean, targeted ESLint clean, check-docs green. _Needs `pnpm prisma db push` (Claude can't) for: `VoucherDetail.shippingTaxAmt` + `TicketOrder.tierDiscount*` + `TicketOrderLine.tierDiscountPaise`. Pre-existing orders invoice at their frozen undiscounted prices (confirmed). - **2026-07-22 — POS shipping charge now carries GST (composite-supply rule)**: the counter-typed shipping amount stays **tax-INCLUSIVE** (customer pays exactly what the cashier typed; no bill total moved), but the embedded GST is now computed, stored, and booked. **Rate rule** (`resolveShippingGstRate` in [stock.ts](src/modules/inventory/lib/stock.ts), used by client AND server): the GST-rate group holding **>50% of the bill's pre-tax, pre-discount goods value** wins; a mixed bill (no group >50%, ties included) takes the **max rate** on the bill; a **service-only bill** applies the same rule over its service rates (repair @18% → shipping @18%); all-exempt/empty → 0%. **Storage:** new `VoucherDetail.shippingTaxAmt` (additive, default 0; `shippingAmt` stays GROSS). **GL** (`buildInvVoucherGlLines`): Shipping Income leg posts **NET** (`shippingAmt − shippingTaxAmt`); the tax joins the existing `chargesTax` pool onto the CGST/SGST/IGST legs — every non-POS caller passes nothing → 0 → byte-identical GL (ecommerce/manual vouchers/purchases/returns untouched; POS returns never carry shipping). **POS screen:** "incl. GST @n% · ₹x" hint beside the Shipping input (display only — server recomputes; no payload change). **Receipt fix (same commit):** the printed CGST/SGST box now pools line tax + **service-charge GST** (was silently goods-only — pre-existing gap) + shipping GST, so the printed tax always equals the books. Gates: tsc clean; resolver harness 8/8 PASS (dominance/tie/fallback/exempt + inclusive back-calc 118→100+18). _Additive schema — needs `pnpm prisma db push` (Claude can't). Parked: ecommerce checkout shipping still untaxed (own change later); estimates/orders inherit at conversion time since /api/pos/sale recomputes._
- **2026-07-18 — POS Services: sell services like products (Settings-defined catalogue, per-service income ledger + GST)**: replaces the bad-UX "+ Add charge" path for service income (kept as-is for odd rows). New **`PosService`** table (`prisma/schema/pos.prisma`: name unique, `accountId` → INCOME-group ledger, `gstRateId` → GstRate, optional tax-inclusive `defaultPricePaise`, sortOrder, isActive) + **Settings → POS Services** page (`/office/settings/pos-services`, [`PosServicesManager`](src/modules/settings/components/PosServicesManager.tsx), CRUD `/api/settings/pos-services[/[id]]` — income-group validated; GST dropdown defaults to the 18% rate). **POS UI:** active services (`GET /api/pos/services`) are added from a **"+ Add service charge" dropdown button in the cart column (Sale mode only)** — NOT the product search box (services have no barcode, so the scan/type dropdown stays products-only). Picking one makes a `kind:"service"` CartLine (`id = −serviceId`, negative so every productId-keyed setter still works); cart renders service rows **after product rows** with a sky tint + Service badge + "GST n%" in place of MRP/Barcode; price entry is tax-inclusive like products. The old raw-ledger "+ Add charge" (GlAccountCombobox) UI was **removed** — the button now adds catalogue services, not raw ledgers. Shipping + Round-off are an always-visible flex row (Adjustments toggle removed); `b.charges` plumbing stays dormant. **Posting (`/api/pos/sale`):** new `services[]` payload (id/qty/tax-EXCLUSIVE unitPrice; server re-resolves ledger + rate — never trusts the client); each becomes a **TAX-BEARING charge** — `ChargeInput.taxAmount` added to `settlementJson` ([settlement.ts](src/shared/lib/accountancy/settlement.ts)) and `buildInvVoucherGlLines` pools `Σ charge.taxAmount` into the CGST/SGST/IGST split while the charge leg posts the NET to the income ledger (additive — no charge carries tax ⇒ byte-identical GL, parity safe). `chargesNet` counts service GROSS so totals/JE balance; services-only sales allowed (`lines` may be empty); services excluded from loyalty earn/redeem base + coupon eval on BOTH sides; stock arm never sees them. Edit round-trip preserves `taxAmount` (vouchers/[id] GET/PATCH + PosScreen editMeta). Order/Estimate **guarded** (toast: Sale-only for now — TODO(pos-services) bridge in PosQuoteScreen). Gates: tsc clean, ESLint clean. _Additive schema — needs `pnpm prisma db push` (Claude can't); not run-tested in the browser (table pending push + auth). Known display note: the web receipt (`/receipt/invoice/[id]`) shows a service as a charge row ("Service: X"), the POS screen/thermal show it as a line. Parked: services on Order/Estimate, SAC codes for the GST classification report, service rows in `hsnSummary`._

- **2026-07-17 — Auto credit-limit calculator (Settings → Credit Limit)**: `Account.creditLimit` (manual, per-customer) now has an optional **auto floor** layered on top. New singleton **`CreditLimitConfig`** (id=1, `prisma/schema/accounts.prisma`): `autoEnabled` (master toggle, default **OFF**), `windowDays` (X, default 90), `pctOfSales` (Y, default 10), optional `maxAutoLimit` + `roundToStep`. **Auto limit = Y% of the customer's POSTED SALE/POS sales in the last X days**; the **effective limit enforced everywhere = max(manual, auto)** — a manual limit never lowers a good auto number and vice-versa. When auto is OFF everything collapses to the old deny-by-default (manual only, null/0 = cash-only). New resolver `src/shared/lib/accountancy/creditLimit.ts` (`computeAutoCreditLimit` / `windowSalesTotal` / `loadCreditLimitConfig` / `resolveEffectiveCreditLimit`); `checkCreditLimit` (POS sale + order fulfil) and the POS `account-snapshot` now resolve through it. **`loadCreditLimitConfig` swallows a missing-table error → auto-OFF**, a temporary bridge so the pre-`db push` window can't 500 POS sales / the Contacts page (remove once the table is live). New **Settings → Credit Limit** page (`/office/settings/credit-limit` + `CreditLimitConfigForm` + `GET/PATCH /api/settings/credit-limit`, entry in `SETTINGS_NAV`). **Contacts list** (`/office/customers`) gained a **credit filter** (Any / Manual limit set / No manual limit — the last uses `NOT` so it never collides with the `q`-search `OR`) + a **Credit limit column** (shows the manual ₹, an "Auto" badge when the global toggle is on, else "—"). Seed: `CreditLimitConfig` singleton in `bootstrap-seed` (create-only, auto OFF). Gates: tsc clean, ESLint clean. _Additive schema — needs `pnpm prisma db push` on the prod DB (Claude can't push); the `orderAdapter` order-board credit badge still reads the manual limit only (parked — its batch doesn't fetch windowed sales). Not yet run-tested in the browser (table pending push)._

- **2026-07-11 — Contacts permissions flattened (radius = branch; `:org` retired) + sidebar-link granular save fix**: the Contacts CRUD grid collapsed from the old `customers:{view,edit,delete}:{own,department,branch,org}` ladder + `customers:manage` umbrella to a **flat set `customers:{view,create,edit,delete}`**. New concept: **radius is only ever branch, decided per object** — Contacts (Person + Company) carry **no branch**, so there is no radius to enforce (any branch may CRUD, gated only by holding the flat write id). `permissionScope.ts` dropped the `customers:manage` legacy-action map; the Contacts matrix row, the person/company/account/contacts-bulk routes, the customer-tools + customer-import gates, the customers/person/companies/customer-retention pages, and `constants.ts` sidebar links all now use the flat ids (`companies:manage` kept — separate concern). Seeds updated (`bootstrap-seed` perm list, `helper-roles` grants). **Also fixed the reported "grant Contacts create/edit not saved" bug**: the Role editor renders a sidebar-link's granular CRUD matrix gated only on the _link_ being in the department pool, but `PUT /api/roles/[id]/permissions` filtered out any id not in the pool — and the pool only carries the `ui:sidebar:*` links, never the granular CRUD, so the ticks were silently dropped. New derived `SIDEBAR_LINK_GRANULAR_IDS` (from the matrix rows) lets the save filter **unlock a link's granular ids once the link itself is pooled** — dept pools _module visibility_, role picks the _granular level_. **DB reconcile (non-destructive):** an idempotent script remapped existing scoped/`manage` grants + pools → flat, then deleted the **13** dead permissions (FK cascade); custom roles preserved (verified 0 deprecated remaining). Gates: every changed route/page module compiles (401/307, no 500). _Parked (its own change): vouchers' same-branch radius enforcement — vouchers HAVE a branch, so a user acts only on their own branch's records; not yet wired (needs the position→branch radius plumbing). Verification gap (dev, accepted): the authenticated Role-editor click-through is unrun (Claude-in-Chrome not connected); mechanism verified via compile + DB state._

(c) **Money boundary** — shared `round2`/`moneyNumber`/`paiseToRupees`/`rupeesToPaise` in `src/shared/lib/format/moneyInr.ts`; `engineGlArm` + `engineCreateVoucher` round every GL line amount + balance delta to 2dp ONCE at the writer (stored line and cached-balance delta are now the same number); local `round2`/`r2` duplicates consolidated (pos/sale route, couponMath, priceLadder, computeGstSplit, giftCard redeem/refund, Gl/JournalVoucherPage). PriceInput); `PosScreen` (426) and `PosQuoteScreen` (278) are now thin mode shells (submit + edit-hydration + checkout wiring only); `CouponHit` moved to `posDraftTypes`. (e) **God-component splits** — TradingVoucherPage: per-kind config + pure line math extracted to `src/modules/accountancy/lib/tradingVoucherKinds.ts` (2,681→2,268; JSX split deferred — 50-state entanglement, low payoff/high risk on the protected form); admin Role page: 2,281→574 page + `src/modules/admin/lib/roleMatrixConfig.tsx` (matrices/types/helpers) + `src/modules/admin/components/RolePageSections.tsx` (the 9 section components). (f) **Hygiene** — junk root files git-rm'd (`eslint`, `service-mgmt@0.1.0`, `tsc-audit.log`, `experiment.env copy.example`); dead deps removed (`@thiagoelg/node-printer`, `node-thermal-printer`, `shadcn`, `xlsx`); PROJECT*BRAIN repo path fixed; AGENTS.md documents accepted legacy folders (`src/utils`/`src/store`/`src/components/ui`). (g) **E2E** — new `e2e/tests/api-guards.spec.ts`: protected APIs 401 anonymous / 200 admin, write route gated, trial balance balances to the paisa. Gates: tsc clean, ESLint 0 errors, doc-sync green. \_Parked for pre-production (user-agreed): backup.sql purge + password rotation, env super-admin removal, Cloudinary-only `images.remotePatterns`, CI workflow (tsc+lint on push), switch `db push`→migrations once schema settles.*
- **2026-06-29 — Online channel voucher types `ONLINE_ORDER` + `ONLINE_INVOICE` (foundation; canon 23→25)**: the locked voucher list was **deliberately expanded to 25** to give the online sales channel its own identity, mirroring how `POS`/`POS_ORDER` were carved out of `SALE`/`SALES_ORDER` purely for Day Book channel badging. Two new `InvVoucherType` members: **`ONLINE_INVOICE`** = the fulfilment sale (same economics as `SALE`/`POS` — stock OUT + GST/GL), **`ONLINE_ORDER`** = the storefront order/commitment (OPTIONAL binding — NO GL, NO stock here; reserves stock via the existing order pipeline at CONFIRMED, fulfils into `ONLINE_INVOICE`). Implemented by adding the two to the scattered per-type classification lists (`bindingForType` OPTIONAL set, `voucherMappers.STOCK_OUT_TYPES`, `postInvVoucher.isPositiveSale`, the post route's `isSale`/`isTradingVoucher`/`isCommitment`, inventory `ALLOWED_TYPES`, `next-ref`, `stock.ts` PREFIX, `seedVoucherSeries` — `OI`/`OO` series) + the recognition layer (`voucherTypeLabel`, `voucherFormUrls`, Day Book kind dropdown/`matchKind`/`OPTIONAL_KINDS`, `VoucherRegisterTable` badges, `online-orders`/`online-invoices` `[id]` detail routes). `ONLINE_INVOICE` shares the SALE path byte-for-byte; `ONLINE_ORDER` mirrors `POS_ORDER`. Gates: tsc clean (exhaustive `Record<InvVoucherType>` maps caught every touchpoint), **parity harness GREEN** (added an explicit `ONLINE_INVOICE` stock-OUT check), bootstrap seed + doc-sync green, `db push`. **Foundation only — created via seed/API this phase.** _Deferred to the ecommerce rebuild:_ storefront/office entry UI, `ShopOrder`→`ONLINE_ORDER` wiring, live reservation, and the `ONLINE_ORDER`→`ONLINE_INVOICE` converter that carries the advance-`RECEIPT` allocation (ADVANCE→AGAINST_REF) so online prepayment auto-settles. Verification gap (dev, accepted): no run-through (types have no UI producer yet).
- **2026-06-29 — POS draft shared across Sale/Order/Estimate (DRY Phase 1)**: the three POS modes are still **two shells** (`PosScreen` = Sale; `PosQuoteScreen` = Order/Estimate by `mode`) navigated by `PosModeSwitch` `<Link>`s — but the in-progress **draft now survives a mode switch**. New `src/store/posDraftStore.ts` (Zustand, **no `persist` — in-memory singleton** so it survives the `<Link>` route change but not a reload / next shift) holds the **shared slice**: `snapshot` (customer) + `cart` + generic bill adjustments (`shippingAmtStr`, `roundOffStr`, `roundOffManuallyEdited`, `charges`). Setters mirror React's `Dispatch<SetStateAction<T>>`, so every existing call site (`setCart((p)=>…)`, `setCart([])`) is **unchanged** — the swap was declaration-only. Shared types extracted to `src/modules/pos/lib/posDraftTypes.ts` (`Snapshot`/`ProductOption`/`CartLine`/`ChargeRow`/`newChargeRow`), de-duplicated from both screens. **Deliberately kept LOCAL** (not in the store): Sale-only money/shift state (tenders, open shift, surplus) and **server-validated money fields** (coupon, loyalty redemption, gift card) — their ₹ value is tied to the exact cart+customer, so carrying them across a switch risks a stale amount (Phase 2 may revisit once each screen re-validates on cart change). **Edit mode is isolated**: both edit-hydration effects `reset()` on mount + unmount so an existing-voucher edit never leaks into (or out of) the carried draft; the new-sale success path still clears fields individually (keeps the customer for a quick re-bill). Gates: `tsc --noEmit` clean, ESLint 0 errors (only pre-existing warnings), doc-sync green. **Verification gap (dev, accepted):** the live click-through (add item → switch mode → cart persists) is unrun — the route is auth-gated and the DB is empty (no seeded user/shift). _Next: Phase 2 — extract the duplicated UI (CustomerPanel / ProductOmnibox / CartLineList / BillTotals) + `useProductSearch`/`useBillMath` so both shells become thin compositions._
- **2026-06-28 — Phase 5: the `UniversalVoucher` spine becomes the sole document; `Voucher`/`VoucherLine` dropped (5E)**: the document layer collapsed onto the GL spine. Every voucher kind is now anchored by **one** `UniversalVoucher` row (`type` spans CONTRA/PAYMENT/RECEIPT/CREDIT*NOTE/DEBIT_NOTE/JOURNAL **and** the inventory kinds), with typed children — `VoucherGlLine` (GL, incl. `role=TENDER` legs), `VoucherInventoryLine` + `InvStockLedger` (goods, re-pointed off the dropped `voucherId` in **5C**), and loose FK-free `VoucherLink` workflow links (ticket/deal/shopOrder/orderPipeline/orderStatus — dual-written in **5A-1**). **Draft = a status flag on the spine** (no separate draft concept); the central `ledgerStatusFilterUv()` gate hides non-POSTED from balances, caches move only on DRAFT→POSTED (**5.0**). **5E** dropped `Voucher` + `VoucherLine` outright (`db push --accept-data-loss`, dev/empty-db hard cutover) — the spine **is** the financial voucher: totals are **computed** from the debit legs (no stored `totalAmount`); party/creator/account are scalar id-only and enriched via lookups. Engine is spine-only (`engineCreateVoucher`→`CreatedVoucher`, `enginePostSpine`; `enginePostVoucher` removed). ~30 readers/routes converted (vouchers list+[id]+lifecycle, POS shifts/close/till-ledger/cash-movements, outstandings, allocations(+open), day-book, statistics, receipt/payment, voucherReceiptSample, refund, convertParty, auditRestore); `userCanVoucher` widened to the spine's string `type`; voucher numbering globally unique (PAYMENT prefix `PV`→`PMT`). Seeds same commit: `purge-vouchers` drops the dead TRUNCATE targets, `dummy-vouchers` stops creating a separate PAYMENT row (spine carries `friendlyRef`). **Gates: tsc clean, parity harness GREEN, recompute-stock ZERO DRIFT.** Restore tags `pre-5e-drop` / `pre-g5-drop`. **5B done (`68f450ed`):** the satellite FKs re-pointed off InvVoucher onto the spine — LoyaltyLot/LoyaltyLedger/LoyaltyMilestoneAward + CouponRedemption + GiftCardTxn `voucherId`→`universalVoucherId`, AdditionalCostAllocation `sourceInvVoucherId`→`sourceUniversalVoucherId`; the loyalty module + coupon/gift-card libs speak spine ids (return path keeps both id faces — spine for loyalty, InvVoucher id for the `refVoucherId` prior-returns graph); cost-pool readers + the AllocateVoucher dialog resolve the picked voucher's spine id. **5D IN PROGRESS — sliced by column-group (each slice green + committed; the all-at-once drop measured ~430 errors / 50 files with no green intermediate, so it's done one column-group at a time).** Design: the spine owns type/reference/date/status/description/party(partyAccountId)/postedAt/lifecycle/createdBy; `InvVoucher`→`VoucherDetail` keeps only its OWN data (frozen partyJson, GST/place-of-supply, printed totals, return links, workflow FKs, settlementJson, branchId). **5D-1 done (`aa2685e2`):** dropped vestigial `InvVoucher.journalEntryId` (== universalVoucherId since G5). **5D-2 done (`358a17fc`):** dropped `InvVoucher.createdById`/`createdBy` — creator on the spine; readers resolve the creator name from `spine.createdById` via batched user lookups. **5D-3 done (`554f72a6`):** dropped the lifecycle METADATA (cancelledAt/By/Reason, deletedById/deleteReason + cancelledBy/deletedBy relations) — who/when/why lives only on the spine; KEPT `status` + `deletedAt` as denormalized filter flags so the ~40 list readers are untouched this slice. **Subtlety found:** cancelFields/deleteFields/restoreFields return objects through a function, so TS's excess-property check did NOT flag the dropped fields — the 3 inventory lifecycle routes would have failed at RUNTIME; fixed each to write only the kept flag. **5D-4 done (`0343e56a`):** dropped `InvVoucher.accountId`/`account` — live party on the spine (`partyAccountId`); ~80 sites converted (WHERE `accountId`→`universalVoucher:{partyAccountId}`, reads→`v.universalVoucher.partyAccountId`, account-includes resolve the party Account from the spine, creates drop it, live party-name search pre-resolves account ids then filters partyAccountId). Frozen partyJson + partyType/partyId stay on the detail. **5D-5 done (`28ee0a97`):** dropped `InvVoucher.description` + `postedAt` — both on the spine; ~38 sites (creates drop them, post/edit/status updates drop postedAt, readers/search resolve description from the spine; where a flow feeds saleToSpec the description is captured in a const so engineGlArm attach-mode keeps the spine description consistent). **5D-6 done (`08d23a0a`):** dropped `InvVoucher.reference` (@unique) — the user-facing number is `spine.friendlyRef`; 127 sites / 40 files (creates drop it; reads→`v.universalVoucher?.friendlyRef`; nested refVoucher/refReturns/invVoucher resolve their own spine's friendlyRef; reference SEARCH→`universalVoucher:{friendlyRef:{contains}}`; invNextRef collision count keys off the spine; saleToSpec inputs pass the spine friendlyRef since engineGlArm attach-mode re-writes it). **NEW trap recorded:** spread responses (`{...voucher}`) SILENTLY drop `reference` (tsc can't flag a missing spread key) — must re-add `reference: spine.friendlyRef` explicitly to every spread response the UI reads. **Voucher numbering DONE (2026-06-28, docs/vocabulary.md):** every voucher holds TWO numbers — **Voucher No** (per-kind string SV-/PMT-/POS-…, field `voucherNo`, renamed from `friendlyRef` in `4a5261c0`) and **Universal Voucher No / UVNo** = the spine's `id` (label `UV-<id>` via `formatUvNo()` in src/shared/lib/accountancy/uvNo.ts). `UniversalVoucher.reference` was DROPPED (`2071dc64`) — the id already uniquely identifies the row so a separate string was unnecessary, and `GL-` wrongly implied GL-only. Display readers show `voucherNo ?? formatUvNo(id)`. The word `reference` is now free for a FUTURE cross-reference column. Engine no longer mints a spine ref; the `GL_POSTING` VoucherSeries is orphaned (optional cleanup). **5D-8 done (status + deletedAt):** dropped both from `InvVoucher` — lifecycle lives ONLY on the spine; ~30 files, tsc clean. Creates drop `status` (spine carries it via engineCreateSpine/enginePost/updateMany); lifecycle routes (cancel/delete/restore/post/hard-delete) load `status`/`deletedAt` from the spine and attach to the in-memory voucher for the LifecycleVoucher helpers, dropping the old `invVoucher.update({status|deletedAt})` writes; readers move `where:{status,deletedAt}` → `universalVoucher:{…}` (MERGE when a `universalVoucher` key already exists, e.g. allocations/open, pos/sale, post route's PHYSICAL_STOCK count); **statistics** groupBy re-homed to the spine (inventory kinds = financial types `notIn`); **pending-stock/vouchers** nested `refReturns.none.status` → `refReturns.none.universalVoucher.status`; **return/search** q-search moved to `universalVoucher.voucherNo` (InvVoucher has no `reference`); **status-toggle route now writes the SPINE status** — the old `universalVoucherId != null` guard was dead (every voucher has a spine now), gated instead on TOGGLEABLE type + non-null spine. A `binding` flag (REGULAR | OPTIONAL) on the header replaces scattered hardcoded type-lists (e.g. TOGGLEABLE): OPTIONAL = non-binding documents (estimate/order, no GL/stock), REGULAR = real transactions (sale/purchase/payment); `type` stays purely descriptive. Branch + workflow FKs still to move off the detail. Derived from `type` at the single spine-create chokepoint via `bindingForType()` (src/shared/lib/accountancy/voucherBinding.ts) in both engine sites (engineCreateSpine + the create-posted path); auditRestore/dummy-seed inherit via snapshot/default. **The status-toggle route now gates on `binding === "OPTIONAL"` instead of the hardcoded `TOGGLEABLE` type-set** (deleted) — `type` is now purely descriptive, the flag carries behaviour. These wheres are now typed `Prisma.InvVoucherWhereInput` so future drift is caught.
